Yet current approaches often adopt two contrasting methods: either relying on fixed update rules with limited cognitive grounding or delegating belief change largely to unconstrained LLM interaction. We introduce ScioMind, a cognitively grounded simulation framework that bridges these paradigms by combining structured opinion dynamics with LLM-based agent reasoning. ScioMind integrates three key components: 1) a memory-anchored belief update rule that modulates susceptibility to influence via personality-conditioned anchoring strength; 2) a hierarchical memory architecture that supports persistent, experience-driven belief formation; and 3) dynamic agent profiles derived from a corpus-grounded retrieval pipeline, enabling heterogeneous personalities, rationales, and evolving internal states. We evaluate ScioMind on multiple case studies in a real-world policy debate scenario. Across metrics including polarisation, diversity, extremization, and trajectory stability, the proposed components consistently yield improvements in behavioural realism. In particular, dynamic profiles increase opinion diversity, memory and reflection reduce unstable oscillation, and anchoring induces persistent belief trajectories that better align with patterns reported in political psychology. These results suggest that our cognitively grounded design provides a novel solution to LLM-based social simulation that improves both stable and behavioural realism

</details>


### [147] [Senses Wide Shut: A Representation-Action Gap in Omnimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13737)
*Trung Nguyen Quang,Yiming Gao,Fanyi Pu,Kaichen Zhang,Shuo Sun,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMAVB基准测试，发现多模态大模型存在“表征-行动鸿沟”：模型内部状态能感知冲突，但输出时难以拒绝错误前提。诊断性干预PGLA可改善这一行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大模型号称感知驱动，但能否检测到文本前提与自身感官输入之间的矛盾（即基础接地能力）尚未被系统测试。

Method: 构建IMAVB基准（500个电影片段，2×2设计：目标模态×前提条件），测试8个开源全模态LLM和Gemini 3.1 Pro，并设计探针引导的logit调整（PGLA）作为干预。

Result: 发现表征-行动鸿沟：隐藏状态编码了冲突信息，但模型输出几乎不拒绝错误前提；存在欠拒绝和过拒绝两种失败模式；接地能力表现出模态不对称性（音频弱于视觉）且对提示工程不敏感。

Conclusion: 全模态接地瓶颈在于从表征到动作的翻译过程而非感知本身，PGLA干预能有效改善拒绝行为。

Abstract: When an omnimodal large language model accepts a question whose textual premise contradicts what it actually sees or hears, does the failure lie in perception or in action? Recent omnimodal models are positioned as perception-grounded agents that jointly process video, audio, and text, yet a basic form of grounding remains untested: catching a textual claim that conflicts with the model's own sensory input. We introduce IMAVB, a curated 500-clip benchmark of long-form movies with a 2x2 design crossing target modality (vision, audio) and premise condition (standard, misleading), which lets us measure conflict detection separately from ordinary multimodal comprehension. Across eight open-source omnimodal LLMs and Gemini 3.1 Pro, we document a Representation-Action Gap: hidden states reliably encode premise-perception mismatches even when the same models almost never reject the false claim in their outputs. Behaviorally, models fall into two failure modes: under-rejection, in which they answer misleading questions as if the false premise were true; and over-rejection, in which they reject more often but also reject standard questions, sacrificing ordinary comprehension accuracy. The gap is modality-asymmetric (audio grounding underperforms vision) and prompt-resistant across seven variants. As an initial diagnostic intervention, a probe-guided logit adjustment (PGLA) re-injects the encoded mismatch signal into decoding and consistently improves rejection behavior. Together, these results suggest the bottleneck for omnimodal grounding lies in translation, not perception.

</details>


### [148] [Harnessing Agentic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13821)
*Jiayi Zhang,Yongfeng Gu,Jianhao Ruan,Maojia Song,Yiran Peng,Zhiguang Han,Jinyu Xiang,Zhitao Wang,Caiyin Yang,Yixi Ouyang,Bang Liu,Chenglin Wu,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AEvo框架，将智能体进化形式化为交互式环境，通过元智能体编辑进化程序或智能体上下文来利用累积证据，在多项基准测试中超越现有方法，取得26%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化方法要么是固定手工设计的模块化流程（灵活度低），要么是通用智能体（长程进化中容易漂移），两者都缺乏稳定的接口来组织累积证据并修正进化机制本身。

Method: 将智能体进化形式化为交互式环境，累积的进化上下文作为过程级状态；引入AEvo框架，其中元智能体观察该状态后，通过编辑控制未来进化的程序或智能体上下文来行动，而非直接提出下一个候选方案。

Result: 在智能体和推理基准上，AEvo超越五种进化基线，相对最强基线提升26%；在三个开放式优化任务中，AEvo进一步超越四种进化基线，在相同迭代预算下达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: AEvo通过统一的元编辑接口，使累积证据在长程搜索中变得可操作，有效指导了基于程序和基于智能体的两种进化范式。

Abstract: Agentic evolution has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving programs, workflows, and scientific solutions by iteratively generating candidates, evaluating them, and using feedback to guide future search. However, existing methods are typically instantiated either as fixed hand-designed procedures that are modular but rigid, or as general-purpose agents that flexibly integrate feedback but can drift in long-horizon evolution. Both forms accumulate rich evidence over time, including candidates, feedback, traces, and failures, yet lack a stable interface for organizing this evidence and revising the mechanism that drives future evolution. We address this limitation by formulating agentic evolution as an interactive environment, where the accumulated evolution context serves as a process-level state. We introduce AEvo, a harnessed meta-editing framework in which a meta-agent observes this state and acts not by directly proposing the next candidate, but by editing the procedure or agent context that controls future evolution. This unified interface enables AEvo to steer both procedure-based and agent-based evolution, making accumulated evidence actionable for long-horizon search. Empirical evaluations on agentic and reasoning benchmarks show that AEvo outperforms five evolution baselines, achieving a 26 relative improvement over the strongest baseline. Across three open-ended optimization tasks, AEvo further outperforms four evolution baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance under the same iteration budget.

</details>


### [149] [History Anchors: How Prior Behavior Steers LLM Decisions Toward Unsafe Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13825)
*Alberto G. Rodríguez Salgado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 前沿LLM作为agent部署时，若历史轨迹中包含有害操作，仅需一句"保持与历史策略一致"的提示，就能使最强对齐模型的违规率从接近0%飙升至91-98%，且模型常会加剧危害程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前前沿LLM在agent场景下，当历史轨迹中包含有害操作时，模型是否会延续有害行为，以及这种延续如何被触发。

Method: 构建HistoryAnchor-100数据集（100个短场景，10个高风险领域），每个场景包含3个强制有害历史动作+1个自由选择节点（2个安全/2个不安全选项）。测试17个前沿模型，对比中性提示与"保持历史一致性"提示的效果，并设置控制实验。

Result: 中性提示下对齐模型几乎不选有害选项；但添加一致性提示后，违规率升至91-98%，且模型常升级危害。控制实验排除标签混淆等解释。不同模型族对有害历史敏感度不同，每个族内旗舰模型受影响最大（安全逆扩展现象）。

Conclusion: 研究结果对agent部署构成严重警示，尤其是轨迹可能被重放、伪造或注入的场景，表明当前对齐方法在agent上下文中存在重大漏洞。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that pick the next action after a long log of prior tool calls produced by the same or a different model. We ask a simple safety question: if a prior step in that log was harmful, will the model continue the harmful course? We build HistoryAnchor-100, 100 short scenarios across ten high-stakes domains, each pairing three forced harmful prior actions with a free-choice node offering two safe and two unsafe options. Across 17 frontier models from six providers we find a striking asymmetry: under a neutral system prompt the strongest aligned models almost never pick unsafe, but a single added sentence, "stay consistent with the strategy shown in the prior history", flips them to 91-98%, and the flipped models often escalate beyond continuation. Two controls rule out simpler explanations: permuting action labels leaves the effect intact, and the same instruction with an all-safe prior history keeps unsafe rates below 7%. Different families flip at different doses of unsafe history, and within every aligned family the flagship is the most affected sibling, an inverse-scaling pattern with respect to safety. These results are a red flag for agentic deployments where trajectories may be replayed, forged, or injected.

</details>


### [150] [Quantifying Sensitivity for Tree Ensembles: A symbolic and compositional approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13830)
*S. Akshay,Chaitanya Garg,Ashutosh Gupta,Kuldeep S. Meel,Ajinkya Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对决策树集成（DTE）的定量敏感性分析方法，通过离散化输入空间并枚举易受敏感性影响的区域，利用代数决策图（ADD）进行高效、可组合、可扩展的计算，工具XCount在实验中显著优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树集成广泛应用于安全关键领域，需要验证模型对输入特征微小变化的敏感性，但现有方法缺乏针对DTE的定量敏感性分析。

Method: 将敏感性分析问题编码为代数决策图（ADD），并将其拆解为可高效求解的子问题，实现组合化和可扩展的计算，在可认证的误差和置信度范围内完成定量敏感性计算。

Result: XCount工具在不同规模和深度的基准测试中，相较于模型计数方法取得了显著的加速，并且能够随集成规模的增大保持良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效、高效地对决策树集成进行定量敏感性分析，XCount工具在性能和可扩展性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Decision tree ensembles (DTE) are a popular model for a wide range of AI classification tasks, used in multiple safety critical domains, and hence verifying properties on these models has been an active topic of study over the last decade. One such verification question is the problem of sensitivity, which asks, given a DTE, whether a small change in subset of features can lead to misclassification of the input. In this work, our focus is to build a quantitative notion of sensitivity, tailored to DTEs, by discretizing the input space of the model and enumerating the regions which are susceptible to sensitivity. We propose a novel algorithmic technique that can perform this computation efficiently, within a certified error and confidence bound. Our approach is based on encoding the problem as an algebraic decision diagram (ADD), and further splitting it into subproblems that can be solved efficiently and make the computation compositional and scalable. We evaluate the performance of our technique over benchmarks of varying size in terms of number of trees and depth, comparing it against the performance of model counters over the same problem encoding. Experimental results show that our tool XCount achieves significant speedup over other approaches and can scale well with the increasing sizes of the ensembles.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Learning When to Act: Communication-Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Run-Time Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12561)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Cody Fleming,Tristan Schuler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李雅普诺夫安全盾牌的强化学习方法，让智能体不仅学习“做什么”，还学习“何时行动”，通过自适应通信时序控制，在倒立摆、小车-杆和平面四旋翼等任务上实现了比传统方法高1.45-3.51倍的平均采样间隔，同时保证了严格的稳定性安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习通常关注智能体应该“做什么”来保证安全，但忽略了“何时”需要采取行动这一关键问题。本文旨在证明单一策略可以联合学习控制输入和通信高效的时序决策，在点态李雅普诺夫安全盾牌下实现安全与通信效率的平衡。

Method: 采用CARE-based LQR备份、李雅普诺夫证书和经典Lyapunov-STC方法，构建运行时间保证(RTA)层，通过一步超前李雅普诺夫预测和预计算的LQR备份来覆盖策略，提供比约束MDP方法更强的安全保证。同时提出基于CARE推导的李雅普诺夫奖励函数，可跨环境迁移而无需重新设计。

Result: 在倒立摆、小车-杆、平面四旋翼上，学习策略的平均采样间隔(MSI)分别达到基线方法的1.91倍、1.45倍和3.51倍；固定LQR控制器在相同平均速率下全部不稳定。RTA盾牌移除后MSI降低1.27-1.84倍且状态范数退化。偏好条件扩展在1/11训练计算量下恢复完整权衡前沿。在12状态3D四旋翼任务中框架可扩展到更高维系统，对±30%质量变化和干扰表现出优雅退化。

Conclusion: 自适应时序而非更低的平均速率使稀疏控制变得安全；CARE导出的李雅普诺夫奖励可跨环境迁移；RTA盾牌对于保证安全至关重要；该方法在离散和连续域上均与算法无关，并可扩展到高维系统，对参数变化和干扰具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) typically asks $\textit{what}$ an agent should do. We ask $\textit{when}$ it needs to act, and show that a single policy can jointly learn control inputs and communication-efficient timing decisions under a pointwise Lyapunov safety shield. We focus on stabilization around a known equilibrium, where CARE-based LQR backups, Lyapunov certificates, and classical Lyapunov-STC are well defined, enabling clean comparison against analytical baselines. A run-time assurance (RTA) layer overrides the policy via a one-step-ahead Lyapunov prediction and a precomputed LQR backup, providing a strictly stronger guarantee than constrained MDP methods that enforce safety only in expectation. On an inverted pendulum, cart--pole, and planar quadrotor, the learned policy achieves $1.91\times$, $1.45\times$, and $3.51\times$ higher mean inter-sample interval (MSI) than a Lyapunov-triggered baseline; a fixed LQR controller at the same average rate is unstable on all three plants, showing that adaptive timing, not a lower average rate, makes sparsity safe. A CARE-derived Lyapunov reward transfers across environments without redesign, with a single weight $w_c$ controlling the stability--communication tradeoff; ablations confirm the RTA shield is essential, with its removal reducing MSI by $1.27$--$1.84\times$ and degrading state norms. A preference-conditioned extension recovers the full tradeoff frontier from one model at $\tfrac{2}{11}$ of training compute, and SAC experiments show the results are algorithm-agnostic across discrete and continuous domains. A 12-state 3D quadrotor case study extends the framework to higher-dimensional systems where classical STC is intractable, and robustness to $\pm30\%$ mass variation and disturbances shows graceful degradation, with the RTA absorbing what the learned policy cannot.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Robust Federated Multimodal Graph Learning under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12584)
*Sirui Zhang,Haonan Wang,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedMPO，一种针对多模态缺失和拓扑隔离的联邦图学习框架，通过拓扑感知生成、缺失感知路由和可靠性感知聚合，在多种缺失和非独立同分布场景下性能提升高达4.10%和5.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多模态图数据常因数据共享限制而孤立存在，且模态往往不完整；现有集中式MGL方法无法处理联邦场景中的知识共享与泛化，而联邦MGL方法主要针对非图数据，缺乏对拓扑隔离和模态缺失的鲁棒处理。

Method: 提出FedMPO框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)拓扑感知跨模态生成——利用全局图上下文恢复缺失特征；(2)缺失感知专家路由——在本地过滤掉噪声重建信号；(3)可靠性感知聚合——根据客户端更新可靠性自适应降低不可靠更新的权重。

Result: 在3个任务、6个数据集上的实验表明，FedMPO优于基线方法，在高缺失率场景下性能提升高达4.10%，在非独立同分布设置下提升5.65%。

Conclusion: FedMPO有效解决了联邦多模态图学习中拓扑隔离下的局部补全和可靠性不均衡的全局聚合两大挑战，为隐私保护下的多模态图学习提供了鲁棒方案。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal graph learning (MGL) has garnered significant attention for integrating diverse modality information and structured context to support various network applications. However, real-world graphs are often isolated due to data-sharing limitations across multiple parties, and their modalities are frequently incomplete. This highlights an urgent need to develop a robust federated approach. However, we find that existing methods remain insufficient. On the one hand, centralized MGL methods that handle missing modalities overlook the knowledge sharing and generalization in federated scenarios. On the other hand, while federated MGL methods have become increasingly mature, they primarily target non-graph data. Based on these technologies, we identify a two-stage pipeline wherein client-side completion reconstructs missing modalities, and server-side aggregation integrates the client-updated parameters of both the modality generator and the backbone models. Although this serves as a general solution, we identify two primary challenges in achieving greater robustness: (1) Topology-Isolated Local Completion: Client-side modality generation struggles to effectively leverage global semantics. (2) Reliability-Imbalanced Global Aggregation: Server-side multi-party collaboration is hindered by client updates with varying modality availability and recovery reliability. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{FedMPO}, which utilizes topology-aware cross-modal generation to recover missing features using comprehensive graph context, missing-aware expert routing to locally filter out noisy recovered signals, and reliability-aware aggregation to appropriately down-weight unreliable updates. Extensive experiments on 3 tasks across 6 datasets demonstrate that FedMPO outperforms baselines, achieving performance gains of up to 4.10% and 5.65% in high-missing and non-IID settings.

</details>


### [153] [OceanCBM: A Concept Bottleneck Model for Mechanistic Interpretability in Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12639)
*Sanah Suri,Kieran Ringel,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OceanCBM是一个用于海洋极端现象（如海洋热浪）预测与物理解析的概念瓶颈模型，通过混合监督学习在保持预测精度的同时实现可解释、物理一致的中间表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法虽能准确预测海洋极端现象，但模型不透明，无法揭示背后的物理驱动机制，且缺乏对真实物理过程的保真度保证。

Method: 提出OceanCBM，一种概念瓶颈模型（CBM），利用混合监督（混合监督）预测混合层热含量，通过预设的物理概念（来自地球物理流体动力学）和一个“自由概念”的中间层路由信息，实现软物理约束而不过度限制模型。

Result: 混合监督能产生一致的机械论表征，而仅用预测或仅用先验约束的基线学习到高度可变的潜在结构；OceanCBM在不牺牲预测性能的前提下实现可解释、物理有根据的表征，并明确刻画了可解释性-性能权衡。

Conclusion: OceanCBM有效弥合了预测精度与物理可解释性之间的鸿沟，为海洋动力学机理诊断提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Extreme ocean phenomena are challenging not only to predict but to diagnose, as accurate forecasts alone do not reveal the underlying physical drivers. While recent machine learning approaches achieve strong predictive skill, they remain largely opaque and provide limited guarantees of fidelity to ground-truth physics. We introduce OceanCBM, the first concept bottleneck model (CBM) for spatiotemporal prediction and mechanistic interrogation of ocean dynamics. OceanCBM uses mixed supervision to predict mixed layer heat content, a key precursor of marine heatwaves, while routing information through an intermediate layer of prescribed concepts derived from geophysical fluid dynamics and a 'free' concept. This design imposes soft physical structure without over-constraining the model, and the free concept both regularizes concept predictions and captures residual physical processes. Across ensemble initializations, we show that mixed supervision yields consistent mechanistic representations, whereas prediction-only and prescription-only baselines learn highly variable latent structures despite similar predictive performance. OceanCBM achieves interpretable, physically grounded representations without sacrificing skill, explicitly characterizing the interpretability-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [154] [Learning to Decide with AI Assistance under Human-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12646)
*Nina Corvelo Benz,Eleni Straitouri,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在AI辅助决策中，AI置信度与人类置信度之间的对齐程度如何影响学习最优决策的复杂度。理论上证明了在完美对齐条件下，学习遗憾可以从Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))降低到O(√(T log T))，并通过两个人类实验验证了理论结果的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明AI置信度与人类置信度的对齐程度与决策效用正相关，但尚未阐明这种对齐对学习最优决策复杂度的影响。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将问题建模为二元预测与二元决策下的双臂在线上下文学习问题（完全反馈），推导遗憾下界；在完美对齐条件下，利用Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式的推广推导上界；通过两个人类实验验证理论鲁棒性。

Result: 下界为Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))；完美对齐时可达O(√(|H|·T log T))，在√(|H|)=O(log T)且B可数时进一步改进至O(√(T log T))。实验表明理论结果在非完美对齐下仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AI与人类置信度的对齐可以显著降低学习最优决策的复杂度，这一结论在真实数据实验中得到验证。

Abstract: It is widely agreed that when AI models assist decision-makers in high-stakes domains by predicting an outcome of interest, they should communicate the confidence of their predictions. However, empirical evidence suggests that decision-makers often struggle to determine when to trust a prediction based solely on this communicated confidence. In this context, recent theoretical and empirical work suggests a positive correlation between the utility of AI-assisted decision-making and the degree of alignment between the AI confidence and the decision-makers' confidence in their own predictions. Crucially, these findings do not yet elucidate the extent to which this alignment influences the complexity of learning to make optimal decisions through repeated interactions. In this paper, we address this question in the canonical case of binary predictions and binary decisions. We first show that this problem is equivalent to a two-armed online contextual learning problem with full feedback, and establish a lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{|H| \cdot |B| \cdot T} )$ on the expected regret any learner can attain, where $H$ and $B$ denote the sets of human and AI confidence values. We then demonstrate that, under perfect alignment between AI and human confidence, a learner can attain an expected regret of $O(\sqrt{|H| \cdot T\log T})$ and, when $\sqrt{|H|} = O(\log T)$ and $B$ is countable, a non-trivial generalization of the Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz inequality improves the regret bound to $O(\sqrt{T\log T})$. Taken together, these results reveal that alignment can reduce the complexity of learning to make decisions with AI assistance. Experiments on real data from two different human-subject studies where participants solve simple decision-making tasks assisted by AI models show that our theoretical results are robust to violations of perfect alignment.

</details>


### [155] [Runtime Monitoring of Perception-Based Autonomous Systems via Embedding Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12651)
*Parv Kapoor,Abigail Hammer,Ashish Kapoor,Karen Leung,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），直接在学习的嵌入空间中进行监控，无需传统低维状态映射，通过嵌入距离定义谓词，结合时序算子表达高层感知概念，经共形校准实现可靠监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时监控依赖将连续传感器观测映射到低维状态变量的离散逻辑命题，但在感知驱动场景中，这种映射需要额外的学习模块，常存在计算开销大、脆弱且语义对齐差的问题。

Method: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），用嵌入空间中的距离定义谓词（如与参考嵌入的相似度），结合时序算子（如"最终""一直"）表达高层感知行为；设计基于嵌入迹的监控器，并用共形校准方法提供可靠且面向安全的谓词评估。

Result: 在多个操作环境中，ETL与真实语义具有强实证一致性，能准确监控时序组合行为。

Conclusion: ETL提供了一种在嵌入空间直接进行时序逻辑监控的新范式，有效解决传统方法在感知驱动系统中的语义鸿沟和计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring of autonomous systems traditionally relies on mapping continuous sensor observations to discrete logical propositions defined over low-dimensional state variables. This abstraction breaks down in perception-driven settings, where such mappings require additional learned modules that are often computationally expensive, brittle, and semantically misaligned. In this work, we propose Embedding Temporal Logic (ETL), a temporal logic that performs monitoring directly in learned embedding spaces. ETL defines predicates through distances between observed embeddings and target embeddings derived from reference observations. This formulation allows specifications to capture high-level perceptual concepts, such as similarity to visual goals or avoidance of semantic regions, that are difficult or impossible to express using traditional predicates. By composing these predicates with temporal operators, ETL naturally expresses temporally extended and sequential perceptual behaviors. We introduce ETL monitors for evaluating specifications over bounded embedding traces, along with a conformal calibration procedure that provides reliable and safety-oriented predicate evaluation. We evaluate our approach across multiple manipulation environments to show that ETL achieves strong empirical agreement with ground-truth semantics, including accurate monitoring of temporally composed behaviors.

</details>


### [156] [Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation via Peer Successes and Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12652)
*Weichen Yu,Xiaomin Li,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Haixin Wang,Yinyi Luo,Chen Henry Wu,Gaurav Mittal,Matt Fredrikson,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOPD是一种利用学生模型在同一提示下的多个采样轨迹（成功与失败）构建更丰富教师信号的新颖蒸馏方法，在编程、数学推理等任务上显著优于传统单轨迹蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于奖励的稀疏信号无法提供细粒度指导，而现有的同策略蒸馏方法（OPD）虽然提供了token级监督，但每个采样轨迹独立处理，忽略了同一提示下多个尝试之间的互信息。作者希望利用学生自身多轨迹的试错行为来构建更信息密集的教师信号。

Method: 提出多轨迹同策略蒸馏（MOPD），核心思想是将学生同一提示下的多个采样轨迹（包含成功和失败）作为同伴上下文，用于条件化教师模型。设计了两种同伴上下文构建方式：正向同伴模仿（仅用成功轨迹）和对比成功-失败条件（同时使用成功和失败轨迹）。教师模型基于这些同伴上下文为学生生成更细粒度的监督信号。

Result: 在竞争性编程、数学推理、科学问答和工具使用等多个基准测试上，MOPD一致优于标准同策略蒸馏基线。进一步分析表明，混合成功-失败上下文能使教师评分更好地与验证者奖励对齐，表明提升源于更忠实、实例自适应的监督。

Conclusion: 有效的同策略蒸馏应当利用学生多轨迹的试错行为，而非将每个采样视为孤立的样本。通过同伴条件化，MOPD能提供更丰富、更自适应的监督信号，从而显著提升模型推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained with sparse verifier rewards, which indicate whether a sampled trajectory succeeds but provide limited guidance about where reasoning succeeds or fails. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers denser token-level supervision by training on student-generated trajectories, yet existing methods typically distill each rollout independently and ignore the other attempts sampled for the same prompt. We introduce Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), a peer-conditioned distillation framework that uses the student's local rollout group to construct more informative teacher signals. MOPD conditions the teacher on both successful and failed peer rollouts: successes provide positive evidence for valid reasoning patterns, while failures provide structured negative evidence about plausible mistakes to avoid. We study two peer-context constructions: positive peer imitation and contrastive success-failure conditioning. Experiments on competitive programming, mathematical reasoning, scientific question answering, and tool-use benchmarks show that MOPD consistently improves over standard on-policy baselines. Further teacher-signal analysis shows that mixed success-failure contexts better align teacher scores with verifier rewards, indicating that the gains arise from more faithful, instance-adaptive supervision. These results indicate that effective on-policy distillation should exploit the student's multi-rollout trial-and-error behavior rather than treating rollouts as isolated samples.

</details>


### [157] [Plan Before You Trade: Inference-Time Optimization for RL Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12653)
*Eun Go,Rohan Deb,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FPILOT框架，一种基于模型预测控制的推理时优化方法，使投资组合管理的强化学习智能体能够在推理时利用价格预测信息，无需重新训练即可提升交易性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的投资组合管理强化学习智能体被训练为静态策略，推理时无法利用价格预测信息，限制了策略的适应性和性能潜力。

Method: 受模型预测控制启发，利用预测模型生成多步价格轨迹（无需动作条件 rollout），构建基于资产配置的想象回报目标，在推理时优化策略后再执行一步交易。

Result: 在TradeMaster DJ30基准上，FPILOT对五种策略学习算法均产生一致改进，提升总收益及Sharpe、Sortino、Calmar等风险调整指标，随机策略受益更多；且性能随预测质量提升而改善。

Conclusion: FPILOT是一种兼容任何预训练智能体的即插即用推理时优化框架，能有效将价格预测融入交易决策，且性能随预测技术进步而持续提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents for portfolio management are typically trained and deployed as static policies, with no mechanism for using price forecasts at inference time. We propose $\text{FPILOT}$ (**Fin**ancial **P**lugin **I**nference-time **L**earning for **O**ptimal **T**rading), a plugin inference-time optimization framework inspired by Model Predictive Control (MPC). Our key structural insight is that future prices mostly do not depend on one agent's portfolio allocation, so a suitable predictive model can produce a multi-step price trajectory without iterative action-conditioned rollouts as in typical reinforcement learning. At each decision step, we use the forecaster's predicted price trajectory to construct an allocation-based imagined return objective, and optimize the policy at inference-time before executing one step of the trade. Our framework is compatible with any pre-trained agent and adapts the policy to the forecaster's predictions without any retraining. Evaluated across five policy learning algorithms on the TradeMaster DJ30 benchmark, $\text{FPILOT}$ produces consistent improvements in total return and return-based risk-adjusted metrics (Sharpe, Sortino, Calmar), with stochastic policies benefiting more than deterministic ones. Further, using synthetic forecasts at calibrated quality levels, we show that gains consistently improve with forecaster quality, suggesting that our performance will improve based on advances in financial forecasting.

</details>


### [158] [scShapeBench: Discovering geometry from high dimensional scRNAseq data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12662)
*Andrew J Steindl,João Felipe Rocha,Brian Tshilengi Di Bassinga,Zachary Warren,Matthew Scicluna,César Miguel Valdez Córdova,Shabarni Gupta,Leire Torices,Daniel Neumann,Timothy J. Mann,Ihuan Gunawan,Dhananjay Bhaskar,John G Lock,Christine L Chaffer,Guy Wolf,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了scShapeBench基准数据集和scReebTower基线方法，用于单细胞数据中自动检测数据结构形状（聚类、轨迹、多分支、原型等），以指导下游分析流程的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞数据分析流程通常假设数据呈特定形状（如聚类、树状、轨迹等），用户需手动判断数据结构后选择工具，这限制了自动化分析。随着AI科学家的兴起，需要自动化的形状检测方法来辅助流程选择。

Method: 构建scShapeBench基准数据集（包含合成数据与专家标注的真实单细胞数据），提出scReebTower方法，利用扩散几何提取Reeb图，将可视化与流程选择相连接，并提供拓扑感知的评估指标。

Result: scReebTower在合成和真实单细胞数据上均优于PAGA和Mapper等现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了基准数据集、评估指标和基线方法，推动了单细胞数据自动形状检测的发展。

Abstract: High-dimensional point cloud data arise across many scientific domains, especially single-cell biology. The shapes or topologies of these datasets determine the types of information that can be extracted. For example, clustered data supports cell-type identification, trajectory structures support transition analysis, and archetypal structures capture continua of cellular behaviors. Existing analysis pipelines often assume a specific shape. The standard Seurat pipeline combines UMAP visualization with Louvain clustering and therefore assumes clustered data, while tools such as Monocle and SPADE assume tree-like structures, and flow-based models such as MIOFlow and Conditional Flow Matching target trajectories. Choosing which pipeline to apply is therefore often left to bioinformaticians who visually inspect datasets before selecting an analysis strategy. With the rise of agentic AI scientists, automating shape detection is increasingly important for selecting downstream analysis pipelines. To address this problem, we introduce scShapeBench, a benchmark dataset for shape detection containing both synthetic and expert-annotated single-cell datasets. Synthetic datasets are sampled from ground-truth skeleton graphs with controlled variance. Real single-cell datasets are curated from diverse sources and annotated by experts into four categories: clusters, single trajectory, multi-branching, and archetypal. We additionally introduce scReebTower, a baseline method that uses diffusion geometry to extract Reeb graphs and connect visualization with pipeline selection. We provide topology-aware evaluation metrics and compare scReebTower against PAGA and Mapper on synthetic and real data. Our results indicate that scReebTower outperforms existing baselines. Overall, our contributions span benchmarks, evaluation metrics, and a baseline for automated shape detection in single-cell data.

</details>


### [159] [ODRPO: Ordinal Decompositions of Discrete Rewards for Robust Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12667)
*Nirmal Patel,Fei Wang,Inderjit Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ODRPO框架，通过将离散奖励分解为序数二元指标，有效隔离评估噪声，提升RLAIF中LLM对齐的鲁棒性，在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B上取得最高14.8%的相对提升，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLAIF中LLM自动评分器存在固有随机性（提示敏感性和采样噪声），标准优势估计器（如GRPO、MaxRL）对噪声敏感，噪声奖励样本会扭曲归一化统计量并破坏全局学习信号。简单增加采样+多数投票虽能降噪但计算成本过高。

Method: 提出Ordinal Decomposition for Robust Policy Optimization (ODRPO)，将离散奖励分解为一系列序数二元指标，独立计算并累积各渐进难度成功阈值上的优势，防止异常值污染全局更新，同时建立隐式的方差感知学习课程。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上，ODRPO相比基线在FACTS-grounding-v2上提升14.8%，在Alpaca-Evals上提升7.5%，且训练时间开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ODRPO为现代RLAIF中带噪声的离散评估环境提供了可扩展且鲁棒的对齐框架，理论分析也证实了其优化稳定性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) utilizes Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback (RLAIF) for non-verifiable domains such as long-form question answering and open-ended instruction following. These domains often rely on LLM based auto-raters to provide granular, multi-tier discrete rewards (e.g., 1-10 rubrics) that are inherently stochastic due to prompt sensitivity and sampling randomness. We empirically verify the stochasticity of auto-raters that can propagate and corrupt standard advantage estimators like GRPO and MaxRL, as a noisy reward samples can skew normalization statistics and degrade the global learning signal. Empirically, sampling more rewards and taking majority voting may reduce the noise and improve performance, but this approach is computationally expensive. To address this bottleneck, we introduce $\textbf{O}$rdinal $\textbf{D}$ecomposition for $\textbf{R}$obust $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{O}$ptimization ($\textbf{ODRPO}$), a framework that structurally isolates evaluation noise by decomposing discrete rewards into a sequence of ordinal binary indicators. By independently computing and accumulating advantages across these progressively challenging success thresholds, ODRPO prevents outlier evaluations from corrupting the global update while establishing an implicit, variance-aware learning curriculum. Empirically, ODRPO achieves robust performance on Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-4B models, outperforming baselines with relative improvements of upto 14.8% on FACTS-grounding-v2 and 7.5% on Alpaca-Evals. Critically, these gains are achieved with negligible training-time overhead, as ODRPO requires no additional compute per step compared to standard estimators. Supported by theoretical analysis confirming its optimization stability, ODRPO provides a scalable and robust framework for aligning models within the noisy, discrete evaluation landscape of modern RLAIF.

</details>


### [160] [A Unified Perspective for Learning Graph Representations Across Multi-Level Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12685)
*Mohamed Mahmoud Amar,Nairouz Mrabah,Mohamed Bouguessa,Abdoulaye Baniré Diallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的多尺度图对比学习框架，结合节点级、邻近级、簇级和图级信息，并引入自适应权重机制来优化正负样本对的相似度权重，无需额外超参数调优，在图分类、聚类和链接预测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自监督学习方法多集中于单一图抽象层次，且对样本对赋予统一惩罚强度，导致优化灵活性不足和收敛状态模糊。

Method: 提出统一对比学习框架，线性组合节点级、邻近级、簇级和图级相似度分数；引入无参数的自适应细粒度权重机制，对偏离目标值的分数赋予更高权重。

Result: 在真实数据集上，该方法在分类、聚类和链接预测等下游任务中，无论在单层次还是多层次场景下，均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该多尺度统一框架结合自适应权重机制有效提升了图表示学习的优化灵活性和性能，同时消除了多任务GSSL中的超参数调优开销。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for generating high-quality representations for graph-structured data. While multi-scale graph contrastive learning has received increasing attention, many existing methods still predominantly focus on a single graph abstraction level. To address this limitation, we propose a unified contrastive framework that can target node-level, proximity-level, cluster-level, and graph-level information and integrate them through a linear combination of similarity scores on positive pairs and dissimilarity scores (i.e., similarity scores on negative pairs). Furthermore, current approaches typically assign uniform penalty strengths to all examples, which reduces optimization flexibility and leads to ambiguous convergence status. To overcome this, we introduce a novel parameter-free fine-grained self-weighting mechanism that adaptively assigns weights to individual similarity and dissimilarity scores. The proposed mechanism emphasizes the scores that deviate significantly from their target values. Our approach not only enhances optimization flexibility but also eliminates the computational overhead of hyperparameter tuning in conventional multi-task GSSL methods. Comprehensive experiments on real-world datasets show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art approaches across downstream tasks, including classification, clustering, and link prediction, in both single-level and multi-level scenarios.

</details>


### [161] [IGT-OMD: Implicit Gradient Transport for Decision-Focused Learning under Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12693)
*Benjamin Amoh,Geoffrey G. Parker,Wesley Marrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IGT-OMD算法，通过隐式梯度传输解决延迟双层级优化中的“陈旧性放大”问题，将传输误差从二次依赖降至线性依赖，首次实现延迟双层级优化的次线性遗憾界，在多个任务上决策损失降低17-55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在决策聚焦学习中，在线设置面临反馈延迟问题：梯度陈旧性与内层求解器敏感性耦合，产生“陈旧性放大”这一独特的双层级优化失败模式，导致遗憾超出单层级延迟理论的预期。

Method: 提出IGT-OMD算法，将隐式梯度传输应用于在线镜像下降框架中，利用存储的内层解在当前参数下重新评估陈旧梯度，将传输误差从二次依赖降至线性依赖，并采用队列长度自适应步长实现次线性遗憾界。

Result: 传输收益在单位延迟时为0.0%（p=1.00），五十轮时单调增长至9.5%（p<0.001）；在LQR、Warcraft最短路径和Sinkhorn最优传输任务上，相比单层级基线减少17-55%的决策损失，且相变与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: IGT-OMD有效解决了延迟双层级优化中的陈旧性放大问题，提供了首个次线性遗憾界保证，实验验证了理论预测的相变行为。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning trains predictive models end-to-end against downstream decision loss, but online settings suffer delayed feedback: outcomes may not arrive for many environment interactions. We identify \emph{staleness amplification}, a failure mode unique to bilevel optimization under delay, in which gradient staleness couples with inner-solver sensitivity to inflate regret beyond single-level delay theory. We prove that any black-box delayed optimizer incurs an irreducible regret cost from inner-solver approximation error, and that gradient staleness contributes a quadratically growing transport error without bilevel-aware correction. Our algorithm, \textbf{IGT-OMD}, applies Implicit Gradient Transport to hypergradients within Online Mirror Descent, re-evaluating stale gradients at the current parameters using stored inner solutions. This method reduces transport error from a quadratic to a linear dependence on delay and achieves the first sublinear regret bound for delayed bilevel optimization with queue-length-adaptive step sizes. Controlled experiments provide a \emph{mechanistic fingerprint}: transport benefit is exactly $0.0\%$ ($p=1.00$) at unit delay and grows monotonically to $9.5\%$ at fifty rounds ($p<0.001$), isolating the correction's effect. On Linear Quadratic Regulator, Warcraft shortest-path, and Sinkhorn optimal transport, IGT-OMD reduces decision loss by $17$--$55\%$ relative to single-level baselines, with phase transitions matching the theory.

</details>


### [162] [UFO: A Domain-Unification-Free Operator Framework for Generalized Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12700)
*Hanli Qiao,George Em Karniadakis,Muhammad Muniruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UFO，一种跨域神经算子框架，通过多域表示的自适应交互实现离散化解耦的算子学习，在多个基准测试中展现出准确、鲁棒的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子大多在单一表示域（物理、频谱或潜在空间）中操作，缺乏跨域交互能力，难以灵活处理不同分辨率和采样模式下的函数映射问题。

Method: 提出UFO框架，通过在不同域上的表示之间进行自适应联合条件交互，实现跨域神经算子学习，支持输入与输出在任意分辨率下的离散化解耦。

Result: 在不连续输入、不规则采样与频谱失配、非线性动力学、随机高频场四个互补基准上，UFO在分布偏移下保持了准确、鲁棒且物理一致的预测。

Conclusion: 跨域、相位调制的实现方式为离散化解耦的神经算子学习提供了强有力的框架。

Abstract: Neural operators have become an effective framework for learning mappings between function spaces, yet most existing architectures realize operators within a single representational domain, such as physical, spectral, or latent space. In this work, we introduce UFO (Domain-Unification-Free Operator), a cross-domain neural operator framework that realizes operators through adaptive, jointly conditioned interactions among representations defined on distinct domains. UFO enables discretization decoupling: the input function can be observed at resolutions or locations different from those used during training, while the solution can be queried at arbitrary output resolutions. Across four complementary benchmarks covering discontinuous inputs, irregular sampling with spectral mismatch, nonlinear dynamics, and stochastic high-frequency fields, UFO delivers accurate, robust, and physically coherent predictions under distribution shifts. These results establish cross-domain, phase-modulated realization as a powerful framework for discretization-decoupled neural operator learning.

</details>


### [163] [Do Fair Models Reason Fairly? Counterfactual Explanation Consistency for Procedural Fairness in Credit Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12701)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有公平性算法虽然能实现结果公平，但可能对不同群体采用不同的推理逻辑，存在“隐藏程序偏差”。本文提出反事实解释一致性（CEC）框架，通过对齐特征归因来检测和缓解这种偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果公平模型虽然满足公平性指标，但仍可能对个体采用根本不同的推理方式，这种“隐藏程序偏差”未被标准公平性指标和算法捕捉到。

Method: 提出反事实解释一致性（CEC）框架：包含最近邻反事实生成方法、改进的积分梯度基线、个体级程序公平性度量及对应的训练损失函数。

Result: 在合成数据、German Credit、Adult Income和HMDA抵押贷款数据上，结果公平基线模型表现出显著的隐藏偏差，而CEC在仅牺牲少量效用的前提下大幅降低了这种偏差。

Conclusion: 提出“机制B”（相同结果、不同推理）作为关键盲区，CEC能有效检测和缓解这一盲区中的程序性公平问题。

Abstract: Machine learning algorithms in socially sensitive domains (e.g., credit decisions) often focus on equalizing predictive outcomes. However, satisfying these metrics does not guarantee that models use the same reasoning for different groups. We show that existing outcome-fair models can still apply fundamentally different reasoning to individuals, a ``hidden procedural bias'' missed by standard fairness metrics and algorithms. We propose Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC), a framework that detects and mitigates this bias by aligning feature attributions between individuals and their counterfactual counterparts. Key contributions include a nearest-neighbor counterfactual generation method, a modified baseline for integrated gradient comparisons, an individual-level procedural fairness metric, and a corresponding training loss. We introduce a taxonomy identifying ``Regime B'' (same outcome, different reasoning) as a critical blind spot. Experiments on synthetic data, German Credit, Adult Income, and HMDA mortgage data demonstrate that outcome-fair baselines exhibit substantial hidden bias, while CEC substantially reduces it with modest utility cost.

</details>


### [164] [Early Data Exposure Improves Robustness to Subsequent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12705)
*Lawrence Feng,Gaurav R. Ghosal,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过上游训练选择（如早期数据暴露）提升模型在后训练中获得的能力在后续微调中的鲁棒性，发现即时性能不能预测保留能力，早期暴露能有效防止遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注下游干预措施来缓解微调中的遗忘问题，但忽视了上游训练选择对能力保留的影响。作者希望从训练阶段主动预防遗忘，而非事后补救。

Method: 在135M和1B参数模型上构建三阶段流水线：预训练、后训练获取目标能力、下游微调。对比不同训练策略（早期暴露、回放、dropout等）对能力保留的影响，并进行计算量匹配实验。

Result: 后训练后的即时性能不能可靠预测后续微调后的能力保留；早期暴露（将后训练数据混入预训练）能持续改善上游性能与下游性能之间的权衡曲线；在计算量匹配实验中，最优分配既不在极端；回放和dropout可与早期暴露互补。

Conclusion: 对后续微调的鲁棒性应作为上游训练的一等目标，应通过早期暴露等预防性策略主动解决，而非等到微调时再反应式干预。

Abstract: How can we train models whose post-trained capabilities survive subsequent fine-tuning? Rather than focusing on downstream interventions to mitigate forgetting of upstream capabilities, we study how upstream training choices - that is, the manner in which a capability is acquired - shape how robustly that capability is retained. We investigate this question in a controlled three-stage language-model pipeline: pretraining, post-training to acquire a target capability, and downstream fine-tuning on a new objective. Across 135M and 1B models, two post-training domains, and two downstream fine-tuning tasks, we find that immediate post-training performance does not reliably predict retention after subsequent fine-tuning: training recipes that look equivalent immediately after post-training can retain the target capability very differently after subsequent fine-tuning. In particular, early exposure - mixing post-training data into pretraining - consistently improves the frontier between retained upstream performance and downstream performance. In compute-matched experiments, where the target data must be allocated between pretraining and post-training, we find that the optimum lies at neither extreme. Together with our other empirical and theoretical findings, this supports the view that post-training drives immediate specialization while early exposure improves robustness to later forgetting. Replay and dropout, typically used to mitigate forgetting as it occurs during fine-tuning, provide complementary gains to early exposure when applied during post-training. Our findings suggest that robustness to subsequent fine-tuning should be treated as a first-class objective of upstream training, addressed preventatively through choices like early exposure rather than reactively during fine-tuning itself.

</details>


### [165] [Layer-wise Representation Dynamics: An Empirical Investigation Across Embedders and Base LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12714)
*Jingzhou Jiang,Yi Yang,Kar Yan Tam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LRD框架，通过三种层间度量（Frenet、NRS、GFMI）分析31个模型在30个MTEB任务上的层表示动态，发现其能揭示架构差异，并在无标签模型选择和推理时层剪枝中有效应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型各层隐藏状态变化显著，但现有层分析仅关注单一变化维度，缺乏统一的层间动态分析框架。

Method: 提出LRD框架，包含三种度量：Frenet（Grassmann流形速度与曲率）度量全局子空间运动、NRS度量局部近邻保持、GFMI度量与最终层的对齐。在31个模型和30个MTEB任务上进行实验。

Result: LRD揭示最终层无法体现的架构与任务差异；模型级得分与下游性能正相关；GFMI在15%和20%剪枝预算下优于随机，Frenet仅在最轻预算有效，NRS无法从选择迁移到剪枝。

Conclusion: 层间结构为模型解释和部署决策提供有效信号，不同度量在不同应用场景各有优势。

Abstract: Hidden states change substantially across the layers of modern language models, but most layer-wise analyses focus on one aspect of that change. We propose Layer-wise Representation Dynamics (LRD), a framework with three layer-wise measurement families: Frenet (Grassmann speed and curvature) for global subspace motion, Neighborhood Retention Score (NRS) for local nearest-neighbor retention, and Graph Filtration Mutual Information (GFMI) for alignment with the final layer. Applying LRD to 31 models (encoder-based and decoder-based embedders, plus base LLMs) on 30 MTEB tasks reveals architectural and task-level differences that are not apparent from final-layer representations alone. We then use LRD for two applications: label-free model selection and inference-time layer pruning. For selection, all three model-level scores correlate positively with downstream MTEB performance, with end-to-end subspace displacement (d_{0,L}) the strongest, and the same direction holds on a smaller base-LLM MMLU panel. For pruning, GFMI is the only measurement-guided rule that beats Random at the 15% and 20% budgets and has the best median change at every budget. Frenet is effective only at the lightest budget, while NRS does not transfer from model selection to pruning. These results show that layer-wise structure provides signal for both interpretation and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [166] [Scaling Laws for Mixture Pretraining Under Data Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12715)
*Anastasiia Sedova,Skyler Seto,Natalie Schluter,Pierre Ablin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型预训练中稀缺目标数据与通用数据混合时的重复利用权衡，发现目标数据可重复使用15-20次，并提出了一个考虑重复效应的混合缩放定律，用于优化数据混合配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型规模扩大，所需数据量激增，但许多目标数据源（如低资源语言或专业领域）天然有限。常用的混合训练策略面临两难：目标数据过少会导致模型对该领域暴露不足，过多则导致重复训练、收益递减甚至过拟合。现有研究缺乏对这一权衡的系统性理解。

Method: 在2000多次语言模型训练实验中，系统研究了多种模型规模、目标数据集大小以及多种数据类型（包括多语言、特定领域和质量过滤混合）。通过分析重复次数对目标域性能的影响，提出了一个考虑重复效应的混合缩放定律，该定律建模了重复目标token价值递减以及通用数据的正则化作用。

Result: 发现重复是影响目标域性能的核心因素，混合训练对重复的容忍度远高于单源训练：稀缺目标语料可重复使用15-20次，最优重复次数取决于目标数据大小、计算预算和模型规模。基于缩放定律的优化可以计算出有效的混合配置。

Conclusion: 验证了在数据受限条件下，通过合理重复使用稀缺目标数据并配合通用数据混合，可以显著提升目标域性能。提出的缩放定律为实际预训练中的数据混合配置提供了理论指导。

Abstract: As language models scale, the amount of data they require grows -- yet many target data sources, such as low-resource languages or specialized domains, are inherently limited in size. A common strategy is to mix this scarce but valuable target data with abundant generic data, which presents a fundamental trade-off: too little target data in the mixture underexposes the model to the target domain, while too much target data repeats the same examples excessively, yielding diminishing returns and eventual overfitting. We study this trade-off across more than 2,000 language-model training runs spanning multiple model and target dataset sizes, as well as several data types, including multilingual, domain-specific, and quality-filtered mixtures. Across all settings, we find that repetition is a central driver of target-domain performance, and that mixture training tolerates much higher repetition than single-source training: scarce target corpora can be reused 15-20 times, with the optimal number of repetitions depending on the target data size, compute budget, and model scale. Next, we introduce a repetition-aware mixture scaling law that accounts for the decreasing value of repeated target tokens and the regularizing role of generic data. Optimizing the scaling law provides a principled way to compute effective mixture configurations, yielding practical mixture recommendations for pretraining under data constraints.

</details>


### [167] [Before the Last Token: Diagnosing Final-Token Safety Probe Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12726)
*Shravan Doda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 最终token安全探针在提示词预填充阶段会遗漏分布在早期用户token表示中的不安全证据，导致对越狱提示的检测失效。本文通过研究这种预填充阶段的失败模式，提出了一种PCA-HMM轨迹模型，能够从用户内容预填充轨迹中恢复被遗漏的检测结果，同时避免过度误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最终token安全探针仅监控单个隐藏状态，但越狱提示中包含分布在早期token表示中的不安全证据，这些证据在最终token读出时被遗漏，导致探针失效。

Method: 使用SafeSwitch风格探针在三个指令微调LLM上训练（仅使用干净的恶意/良性提示）；进行子空间分析研究探针表征空间的局限性；对token级预填充进行逐位置分析；提出PCA-HMM轨迹模型从用户内容预填充轨迹中恢复检测。

Result: 探针对干净恶意提示召回率高，但遗漏许多越狱提示并在安全相关良性提示上产生误报。子空间分析表明被遗漏的越狱与干净良性提示在探针表征空间方向上有差异，增加探针瓶颈宽度无法可靠解决。token级分析显示不安全证据常在序列早期出现但不在最终token读出，而简单的最大池化会导致过度误报。PCA-HMM轨迹模型能恢复许多遗漏检测且无灾难性误报。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的隐藏状态分析可作为最终token探针的诊断补充，PCA-HMM轨迹模型是一种有效且实用的方法。

Abstract: Final-token safety probes monitor a single hidden state after prompt prefill, but jailbreak prompts can contain probe-visible unsafe evidence distributed across earlier user-token representations that is missed by this readout. We study this prefill-time failure mode using SafeSwitch-style probes trained only on clean harmful and benign prompts across three instruction-tuned LLMs. The probes achieve high recall on clean harmful prompts, but miss many jailbreaks and can produce false positives on safety-adjacent benign prompts. Subspace analyses suggest that missed jailbreaks differ from clean benign prompts along directions that are poorly captured by the probe's representational subspace, and increasing probe bottleneck width does not reliably resolve this mismatch. Token-level prefill analyses reveal that probe-visible unsafe evidence often appears earlier in the sequence but is not exposed at the final-token readout, while naive max-pooling over token positions overfires on safe prompts. A simple PCA-HMM trajectory model, trained only on the same clean split, recovers many final-token misses from user-content prefill trajectories without the catastrophic false-positive behavior of naive token pooling, motivating trajectory-aware hidden-state analyses as diagnostic complements to final-token probes

</details>


### [168] [From Generalist to Specialist Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12733)
*Yujia Zheng,Fan Feng,Yuke Li,Shaoan Xie,Kevin Murphy,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在完全非参数设定下，证明了跨时间步的任务结构可识别，以及每个时间步内任务相关潜在表示可通过稀疏正则化实现可识别，为通用模型向专用模型转变提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型需要学习任务相关的专用表示，而可识别性是保证模型能恢复真实表示的渐近极限，对下游应用至关重要。

Method: 采用完全非参数方法，不依赖干预、参数形式或结构约束。首先证明跨时间步任务结构的无监督可识别性，然后利用稀疏正则化实现每个时间步内任务相关潜在表示与无关部分的解耦。

Result: 建立了层次化的可识别性基础：跨时间步的任务结构可识别，每个时间步内任务相关潜在表示可识别。两项结果均为首次在非参数设定下给出通用可识别性保证。

Conclusion: 为从通用模型向专用模型的可证明转变迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Given a generalist model, learning a task-relevant specialist representation is fundamental for downstream applications. Identifiability, the asymptotic guarantee of recovering the ground-truth representation, is critical because it sets the ultimate limit of any model, even with infinite data and computation. We study this problem in a completely nonparametric setting, without relying on interventions, parametric forms, or structural constraints. We first prove that the structure between time steps and tasks is identifiable in a fully unsupervised manner, even when sequences lack strict temporal dependence and may exhibit disconnections, and task assignments can follow arbitrarily complex and interleaving structures. We then prove that, within each time step, the task-relevant latent representation can be disentangled from the irrelevant part under a simple sparsity regularization, without any additional information or parametric constraints. Together, these results establish a hierarchical foundation: task structure is identifiable across time steps, and task-relevant latent representations are identifiable within each step. To our knowledge, each result provides a first general nonparametric identifiability guarantee, and together they mark a step toward provably moving from generalist to specialist models.

</details>


### [169] [ConRetroBert: EMA Stabilized Dual Encoders for Template-Based Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12736)
*Mohammad Jahid Ibna Basher,Ali Khodabandeh Yalabadi,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Ozmen Garibay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConRetroBert提出了一种基于双编码器框架的模板逆向合成方法，通过密集检索+列表排序替代传统全局分类，在USPTO-50k上达到62.4% top-1准确率，微调后达75.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板方法在逆向合成中因全局长尾分类策略而逊于无模板方法，作者认为这是学习范式的问题而非模板本身的问题。

Method: 双编码器框架：阶段1用对比预训练学习产物与模板共享嵌入空间；阶段2用多正例列表排序目标优化模板排序，采用慢速EMA模板编码器稳定难负样本挖掘。

Result: Stage 2排序将top-1准确率从50.5%提升至61.3%，EMA稳定化进一步达到62.4%；微调后达75.4%。在长尾稀有模板上表现优异。

Conclusion: 检索式模板预测能有效克服模板方法的长尾分类弱点，且许多正确预测来自替代模板而非仅真实标签。

Abstract: Template based single step retrosynthesis predicts reactants by selecting and applying an explicit reaction template, making each prediction traceable to a chemical transformation rule. This is useful for synthesis planning, but template based methods are often viewed as less competitive than template free models because template prediction is commonly formulated as global classification over a long tailed rule library. We argue that this weakness is not inherent to templates, but to the learning formulation. We present ConRetroBert, a dual encoder framework that reframes template based retrosynthesis as dense product template retrieval followed by candidate set listwise ranking. Stage 1 uses contrastive pretraining to learn a shared embedding space between products and reaction templates. Stage 2 refines template ranking over mined hard negative candidate sets with a multi positive listwise objective. To enable template side adaptation without destabilizing hard negative mining, ConRetroBert uses a slow moving exponential moving average template encoder for retrieval bank construction while updating the live template encoder through the ranking loss. On the local USPTO-50k benchmark, Stage 2 candidate set ranking improves top-1 reaction accuracy from 50.5% to 61.3%, while EMA stabilized template adaptation further improves it to 62.4%. Fine tuning from a leakage controlled USPTO-Full checkpoint reaches 75.4% top-1 accuracy on USPTO-50k. We also show that retrieval based template prediction is strong in the long tail of rare templates, and that many correct reactant predictions arise from alternative explicit templates rather than only the recorded positive label. Code and data are available at https://github.com/JahidBasher/ConRetroBert.

</details>


### [170] [Learning with Rare Success but Rich Feedback via Reflection-Enhanced Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12741)
*Yuwei Zhang,Sha Li,Changlong Yu,Qin Lu,Shuowei Jin,Chengyu Dong,Haoran Liu,Ilgee Hong,Xintong Li,Zhenyu Shi,Bing Yin,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反思增强自蒸馏（RESD），将失败反馈转化为主动纠正信号，显著提升LLM在稀有成功场景下的持续学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法将环境反馈视为被动条件信号，在成功轨迹稀少的场景下学习困难，需要更高效利用失败经验的方法。

Method: RESD通过生成回顾性反思诊断局部错误，并构建全局持久剧本保留可复用的经验教训，将失败反馈转化为token级别的主动监督信号。

Result: 在多个持续学习任务上显著优于标准自蒸馏基线；相比GRPO，仅用单次rollout即可实现8倍样本效率的早期改进。

Conclusion: RESD通过主动利用失败反馈，在稀有成功场景下比现有方法具有更高的交互效率和学习能力。

Abstract: Enabling Large Language Models (LLMs) to continuously improve from environmental interactions is a central challenge in post-training. While on-policy self-distillation offers a promising paradigm, existing methods predominantly treat environmental feedback as a passive conditioning signal. Consequently, they heavily rely on successful demonstrations and struggle to learn in rare-success regimes. To bridge this gap, we introduce Reflection-Enhanced Self-Distillation (RESD), a framework that transforms raw failure feedback into an active source of corrective supervision. Instead of passively appending feedback, RESD interprets failed trajectories by generating retrospective reflections to diagnose local errors, and curates a persistent global playbook to preserve reusable lessons across training steps. The enriched context enables the self-teacher to provide actionable token-level supervision even in the absence of successful rollouts. Empirical evaluations on multiple continual learning tasks demonstrate that RESD substantially outperforms standard self-distillation baselines. Furthermore, RESD achieves significantly faster early-stage improvement than GRPO with $8\times$ samples using only a single rollout per prompt, highlighting its superior interaction efficiency.

</details>


### [171] [Low-Rank Adapters Initialization via Gradient Surgery for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12752)
*Joana Pasquali,Ramiro N. Barros,Arthur S. Bianchessi,Vinícius Conte Turani,João Vitor Boer Abitante,Rafaela Cappelari Ravazio,Christian Mattjie,Otávio Parraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLICE是一种基于梯度手术的LoRA适配器初始化方法，用于缓解大型语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，通过投影算子调和当前任务与回放缓冲区的梯度，并用截断SVD初始化适配器权重，在标准与对抗性序列上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在持续微调中虽具有参数高效、任务模块化等优势，但灾难性遗忘问题依然严重，其严重程度取决于连续任务梯度的交互方式——当任务梯度冲突时，标准适配器初始化会将更新通道导向覆盖先前学习方向的子空间。

Method: SLICE通过梯度手术（gradient surgery）积累当前任务和回放缓冲区的梯度，利用投影算子调和冲突梯度，再通过截断SVD分解来初始化LoRA适配器权重。

Result: 在TRACE基准和Super-NI任务序列（包括人为构造的梯度对抗序列）上，SLICE相比vanilla LoRA、LoRA-GA和LoRAM，在平均性能、最终性能和遗忘指标上均有改进，同时保持了泛化性能和上下文性能，实现了更好的稳定性-可塑性平衡。

Conclusion: SLICE通过梯度手术式的初始化策略有效缓解了LoRA在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在标准与对抗性持续学习序列上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: LoRA is widely adopted for continual fine-tuning of Large Language Models due to its parameter efficiency, modularity across tasks, and compatibility with replay strategies. However, LoRA-based continual learning remains vulnerable to catastrophic forgetting, whose severity depends on how successive task gradients interact: when consecutive task gradients conflict, standard adapter initializations channel updates into subspaces that overwrite previously learned directions. We propose SLICE, a gradient-surgery-based initialization for LoRA adapters in continual learning. SLICE accumulates gradients from both the current task and a replay buffer of prior tasks, reconciles them through a projection operator, and decomposes the result via truncated SVD to initialize the adapter weights. We evaluate SLICE on the TRACE benchmark and sequences of Super-NI tasks, including a set of adversarial Super-NI sequences that we construct by mining task pairs with maximally opposing gradients. Compared to vanilla LoRA, LoRA-GA, and LoRAM, SLICE consistently achieves a better stability-plasticity trade-off, improving Average Performance, Final Performance and Forgetting metrics while preserving General Performance and In Context Performance across both standard and adversarial continual learning sequences.

</details>


### [172] [Constraint-Aware Flow Matching: Decision Aligned End-to-End Training for Constrained Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12754)
*Jacob K. Christopher,James E. Warner,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出约束感知流匹配（Constraint-Aware Flow Matching），一种端到端框架，通过将约束投影显式纳入训练目标，解决了现有约束生成模型中训练与采样不匹配的问题，在三个真实基准测试上验证了其通用性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型在融入物理约束时，训练自由的约束采样方法虽能保证每个样本的可行性，但导致训练目标与约束采样过程之间存在根本性不匹配，从而引发性能下降。

Method: 提出Constraint-Aware Flow Matching，将约束投影显式整合到训练目标中，使模型学习的动力学与约束采样过程对齐，从而缓解投影校正带来的分布偏移。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的真实世界基准测试上取得了良好效果，展示了方法的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过对齐训练与采样过程，该方法能够生成高质量的约束样本，克服了现有方法中训练-采样不匹配的核心限制。

Abstract: Deep generative models provide state-of-the-art performance across a wide array of applications, with recent studies showing increasing applicability for science and engineering. Despite a growing corpus of literature focused on the integration of physics-based constraints into the generation process, existing approaches fail to enforce strict constraint satisfaction while maintaining sample quality. In particular, training-free constrained sampling methods, while providing per-sample feasibility guarantees, introduce a fundamental mismatch between the training objective and the constrained sampling procedure, often leading to performance degradation. Identifying this training-sampling misalignment as a central limitation of current constrained generative modeling approaches, this paper proposes Constraint-Aware Flow Matching, a novel end-to-end framework that explicitly incorporates constraint projections into the training objective. By aligning the model's learned dynamics with the constrained sampling process, the proposed method mitigates distributional shift induced by projection-based corrections, enabling high-quality constrained generation. The proposed approach is evaluated on three challenging real-world benchmarks, illustrating the generality and efficacy of the method.

</details>


### [173] [Inference-Time Machine Unlearning via Gated Activation Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12765)
*Vinícius Conte Turani,Otávio Parraga,João Vitor Boer Abitante,Kristen K. Arguello,Joana Pasquali,Ramiro N. Barros,Flavio du Pin Calmon,Christian Mattjie,Rodrigo C. Barros,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUARD-IT是一种无需训练和梯度的推理时门控激活重定向方法，通过输入相关的激活旋转实现机器反学习，在TOFU和MUSE上匹配或超越12种梯度方法，支持持续反学习且量化下仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的机器反学习方法计算成本高、导致不可逆权重变化，且模型量化后性能下降；而简单的激活工程使用全局固定向量，对不同输入施加相同干预，导致意外的行为改变。

Method: 提出GUARD-IT，一种推理时门控激活重定向方法。通过在残差流中施加保范旋转实现输入相关的激活干预，无需训练和梯度计算，不修改模型权重。

Result: 在TOFU和MUSE数据集上，跨越三种模型规模，GUARD-IT匹配或超越了12种基于梯度的基线方法。它是唯一能同时保持效用、抑制记忆、避免灾难性崩溃的方法，支持持续反学习，且在量化条件下仍然有效。

Conclusion: GUARD-IT为机器反学习提供了一种高效、灵活且鲁棒的替代方案，克服了梯度方法和简单激活工程的局限性，特别适用于模型量化和持续反学习等实际场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models memorize vast amounts of training data, raising concerns regarding privacy, copyright infringement, and safety. Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of a targeted forget set while preserving model performance, ideally approximating a model retrained from scratch without the forget set. Existing approaches aim to achieve this by updating model parameters via gradient-based methods. However, these updates are computationally expensive, lead to irreversible weight changes, and degrade when the model is quantized for deployment. A recent alternative to changing model weights is activation engineering, where activations are changed during inference to steer model behavior. Despite circumventing weight editing, naive activation steering introduces its own failure modes, as a single global steering vector applies the same intervention to every input, leading to unintended changes in model behavior. We introduce Inference-Time Unlearning via Gated Activation Redirection (GUARD-IT), a training- and gradient-free method that unlearns via input-dependent activation steering at inference time. The resulting intervention is applied as a norm-preserving rotation in the residual stream, leaving model weights untouched. Experiments on TOFU and MUSE show that GUARD-IT matches or exceeds 12 gradient-based baselines across three model scales, while being the only method to simultaneously preserve utility, suppress memorization, and avoid catastrophic collapse across all settings. GUARD-IT further supports continual unlearning without retraining, and remains effective under quantization, a scenario in which parameter-editing methods degrade.

</details>


### [174] [WriteSAE: Sparse Autoencoders for Recurrent State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12770)
*Jack Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WriteSAE，首个能分解和编辑状态空间/混合循环语言模型矩阵缓存写入的稀疏自编码器，通过因子化解码器原子为原生写入形状，实现单槽替换和行为编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差SAE只能读取残差流，无法触及Gated DeltaNet、Mamba-2、RWKV-7等模型的$d_k \times d_v$缓存写入（通过秩-1更新$k_t v_t^\top$），需要一种能直接分解和编辑该写入过程的SAE。

Method: WriteSAE将每个解码器原子分解为原生写入形状，导出逐token logit偏移的闭式解，在匹配Frobenius范数下训练，使得原子一次替换一个缓存槽位。

Result: 原子替换在Qwen3.5-0.8B L9 H4的92.4%触发上胜出（n=4,851），87原子群体测试89.8%胜出，闭式解预测效果$R^2=0.98$，Mamba-2-370M在88.1%触发上成功。持续三位安装使目标延续的midrank从33.3%提升至100%（贪心解码）。

Conclusion: 首次在矩阵递归写入位点实现行为层面的安装编辑，证明了WriteSAE能有效分解和编辑混合循环语言模型的缓存写入。

Abstract: We introduce WriteSAE, the first sparse autoencoder that decomposes and edits the matrix cache write of state-space and hybrid recurrent language models, where residual SAEs cannot reach. Existing SAEs read residual streams, but Gated DeltaNet, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7 write to a $d_k \times d_v$ cache through rank-1 updates $k_t v_t^\top$ that no vector atom can replace. WriteSAE factors each decoder atom into the native write shape, exposes a closed form for the per-token logit shift, and trains under matched Frobenius norm so atoms swap one cache slot at a time. Atom substitution beats matched-norm ablation on 92.4% of $n=4{,}851$ firings at Qwen3.5-0.8B L9 H4, the 87-atom population test holds at 89.8%, the closed form predicts measured effects at $R^2=0.98$, and Mamba-2-370M substitutes at 88.1% over 2,500 firings. Sustained three-position installs at $3\times$ lift midrank target-in-continuation from 33.3% to 100% under greedy decoding, the first behavioral install at the matrix-recurrent write site.

</details>


### [175] [ToolMol: Evolutionary Agentic Framework for Multi-objective Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12784)
*Andrew Y. Zhou,Sharvaree Vadgama,Sumanth Varambally,Peter Eckmann,Michael K. Gilson,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ToolMol 是一个结合多目标遗传算法与 LLM 智能体操作的进化框架，利用 RDKit 工具箱实现精确分子修饰，在多目标性质优化和结合亲和力预测上超越现有方法，生成更有效、可合成的药物候选分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的分子生成方法常产生大量无效或低质量的配体候选物，主要原因是模型在处理分子字符串时存在语法局限性。

Method: 提出 ToolMol 框架，将多目标遗传算法与 LLM 智能体操作相结合，利用 RDKit 支持的综合工具箱让智能体进行精确的配体修饰，通过迭代更新配体群体进行药物设计。

Result: 在多目标性质优化任务上达到 SOTA；生成的配体预测结合亲和力比现有方法强 10% 以上；在绝对结合自由能评分上超过现有方法 35% 以上。

Conclusion: 工具调用使模型更忠实地执行计划中的分子修饰，有效利用了 LLM 中强大的化学先验知识。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have recently opened new and promising avenues for small-molecule drug discovery. Yet existing LLM-based approaches for molecular generation often suffer from high rates of invalid and low-quality ligand candidates, a result of the syntactic limitations of current models with regard to molecular strings. In this paper, we introduce $\texttt{ToolMol}$, an evolutionary agentic framework for de novo drug design. $\texttt{ToolMol}$ combines a multi-objective genetic algorithm with an agentic LLM operator that iteratively updates the ligand population. We build a comprehensive toolbox of RDKit-backed functions that allows our agentic operator to consisently make precise ligand modifications. $\texttt{ToolMol}$ achieves state-of-the-art performance on multi-objective property optimization tasks, discovering drug-like and synthesizable ligands that have $>10\%$ stronger predicted binding affinity compared to existing methods, evaluated on three protein targets. $\texttt{ToolMol}$ ligands additionally achieve state-of-the-art results in gold-standard Absolute Binding Free Energy scores, gaining over existing methods by over $35\%$. By studying chain-of-thought reasoning traces, we observe that tool-calling enables the model to more faithfully execute its planned modifications, efficiently exploiting the strong chemical prior knowledge in LLMs.

</details>


### [176] [Emergent and Subliminal Misalignment Through the Lens of Data-Mediated Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12798)
*Baris Askin,Muhammed Ustaomeroglu,Anupam Nayak,Gauri Joshi,Guannan Qu,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将大模型微调后的“涌现性错位”（Emergent Misalignment）理解为一种数据中介的迁移现象：有害微调数据并非导致均匀的行为溢出，而是与数据集的结构特性、任务难度、预训练分布及训练管道相互作用，并通过“潜意识学习”（Subliminal Learning）在看似无害的数据中传递错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究观察到在窄域有害数据集上微调LLM会引发超出微调分布范围的涌现性错位行为，但其背后的机制尚不清晰，需要更系统的理解来解释这一现象为何发生、如何传播。

Method: 通过系统性实验，分析微调数据与评估提示之间的功能结构相似性、提示的可利用空间、目标行为的学习可靠性、预训练组成的影响，并首次在SFT之外比较离策略和在线策略蒸馏下错位的传递方式。

Result: 错位更容易出现在：微调与评估提示具有相似功能结构时、提示留有更多生成有害内容的余地时、目标行为已被模型更可靠地学习时。预训练组成也影响后续错位；潜意识学习可通过有害教师生成的看似良性数据传递错位；不同蒸馏策略下错位传播机制存在差异。

Conclusion: 涌现性与潜意识性错位不应被视为孤立有害微调样本的简单后果，而是微调数据结构、预训练分布和训练管道三者相互作用的结果，应采用以数据为中心的视角来理解和应对。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs on narrow harmful datasets can induce Emergent Misalignment (EM), where models exhibit misaligned behavior far beyond the fine-tuning distribution. We argue that emergent misalignment can be better understood as a data-mediated transfer phenomenon: harmful fine-tuning examples do not induce uniform behavioral spillover, but interact with the structural properties of the dataset and the difficulty of the tasks relative to the model. Across our experiments, we find that misalignment appears more readily when fine-tuning and evaluation prompts share similar underlying functional structure, when prompts leave more room for coherent harmful completions, and when the target behavior has been more reliably learned by the model. The training pipeline itself also matters: pretraining composition shapes later misalignment. We further study Subliminal Learning (SL), where misalignment is transmitted by fine-tuning on seemingly benign data generated by a harmful teacher. Moving beyond the standard SFT setting, we for the first time compare this transfer under off-policy and on-policy distillation as well, allowing us to separate the roles of the teacher guidance and the training data distribution in transmitting misalignment. Together, these results argue for a data-centric view: Emergent/subliminal misalignment should not be treated as a simple consequence of isolated harmful fine-tuning examples, but as the result of interactions between fine-tuning data structure, pretraining distributions, and training channels.

</details>


### [177] [Discrete MeanFlow: One-Step Generation via Conditional Transition Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12805)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Md Sajid Ahmed,Ruiquan Huang,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Discrete MeanFlow，将连续空间中的一步生成方法扩展到离散状态空间，通过学习有限状态上的条件转移核实现单步离散生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续MeanFlow依赖平滑轨迹和空间导数，无法直接应用于离散状态空间，因此需要为离散空间设计等效的一步生成框架。

Method: 用连续时间马尔可夫链的条件转移核替代连续流中的速度场，提出平均离散率概念，证明Discrete MeanFlow恒等式，并采用边界保证设计直接参数化转移核。

Result: 在精确有限状态马尔可夫链上，学习到的核能以高精度恢复解析真解；在合成序列生成任务上，不同字母表大小和序列长度下均有效。

Conclusion: Discrete MeanFlow实现了离散空间的一步生成，无需迭代去噪、ODE积分或多步精炼，为离散生成提供了一种高效的新方法。

Abstract: MeanFlow enables one-step generation in continuous spaces by learning an average velocity over a time interval rather than the instantaneous velocity field of flow matching. However, discrete state spaces do not have smooth trajectories or spatial derivatives, so the continuous formulation does not directly apply. We introduce Discrete MeanFlow, which replaces the motion of a point with the transport of probability mass over finite states. Our key object is the conditional transition kernel of a continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC), from which we define a mean discrete rate that measures the average change in transition probability over a time interval. We prove a Discrete MeanFlow identity that relates this finite-interval rate to the instantaneous CTMC generator at the endpoint, with the Kolmogorov forward equation replacing the spatial chain rule of continuous MeanFlow. Based on this identity, we parameterize the transition kernel directly using a boundary-by-construction design that guarantees valid probability outputs and exact boundary conditions without auxiliary losses. Since the learned kernel is itself a probability distribution, generation reduces to a single forward pass followed by one categorical draw meaning no iterative denoising, ODE integration, or multi-step refinement is required. We validate the framework on exact finite-state Markov chains, where the learned kernel recovers the analytical ground truth to high precision, and on factorized synthetic sequence generation tasks with varying alphabet sizes and sequence lengths.

</details>


### [178] [Neurodata Without Boredom: Benchmarking Agentic AI for Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12808)
*Ling-Qi Zhang,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用AI智能体解决神经科学数据碎片化与格式不统一的问题。通过让通用编码AI智能体处理8篇近期论文中不同类型的小鼠神经群体记录数据（包括NWB、专用API、通用Python/MATLAB文件等格式），评估其将数据加载、理解并重新格式化以用于神经活动解码任务的能力。结果表明，AI智能体在子任务上表现良好，但难以实现完全无误的端到端解决方案；同时发现AI智能体作为评判者不可靠，仍需人工参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学数据高度碎片化，不同实验室、格式和实验范式之间的数据重用需要大量人工工作。现有的通用数据格式在灵活性与描述性之间存在根本矛盾：过于灵活则难以自解释，过于描述性则要求大量文档和人工标注。因此，需要探索AI智能体是否能够自动解决数据格式转换这一难题。

Method: 选取8篇近期发表的小鼠大规模神经群体记录论文（涵盖多种记录模态、行为范式和数据格式），向AI智能体提供数据、代码和论文原文，要求其加载、理解并重新格式化数据，完成"从神经活动解码任务或行为变量"这一通用下游任务。分析智能体在各子任务上的表现、错误类型及其触发条件，并提出面向AI智能体时代的数据共享最佳实践。

Result: 通用编码AI智能体在单项子任务上表现良好，但很少能完全无误地完成整个端到端流程。智能体作为错误评判者并不可靠，尤其缺乏真实参照的情况下；因此交互式的人工参与编码仍属必要。

Conclusion: AI智能体在自动化处理神经科学数据格式转换方面展现出潜力，但目前尚不能完全替代人工；研究者提出面向AI智能体时代的数据共享最佳实践，以提升数据的机器可读性和可重用性。

Abstract: Neuroscience data are highly fragmented across labs, formats, and experimental paradigms, and reuse often requires substantial manual effort. A persistent roadblock to data reuse and integration is the need to decipher bespoke and diverse data formatting choices. Common data formats have been proposed in response, but the field continues to struggle with a fundamental tension: formats flexible enough to accommodate diverse experiments are rarely descriptive enough to be self-explanatory, and sufficiently descriptive formats demand detailed documentation and curation effort that few labs can sustain. Agentic AI is a natural candidate to solve this problem: LLMs read code and text faster and with sustained attention to the low-level details humans tend to skim over. To measure how well agentic AI performs on this task, we selected eight recent papers studying large-scale mouse neural population recordings that shared both data and code, spanning diverse recording modalities, behavioral paradigms, and dataset formats (e.g., NWB, specialized APIs, and general-purpose Python or MATLAB files). We provided agents with the data, code, and paper, and prompted them to load, understand, and reformat the data for a common downstream task: training a decoder from neural activity to task or behavioral variables. General-purpose coding agents commonly used by scientists performed well on each sub-task, but rarely strung together a fully error-free end-to-end solution. We characterize the types of mistakes agents made and the dataset properties that elicited them, and propose data-sharing best practices for the agentic-AI era. We further find that agents-as-judges are unreliable at catching errors, especially without ground-truth references, so interactive, human-in-the-loop coding remains necessary.

</details>


### [179] [Correcting Influence: Unboxing LLM Outputs with Orthogonal Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12809)
*Shixing Yu,Promit Ghosal,Kyra Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在中介方法的框架，通过稀疏自编码器和雅可比向量积，实现大语言模型在医疗等高风险领域中预测结果的Token级训练数据归因，提升模型可信度和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影响力函数方法局限于自回归设置，且依赖Token独立性的隐含假设，导致归因结果不可靠；医疗等高风险领域需要Token级别的精确归因以增强模型可信度。

Method: 在预训练LLM的任意层附加稀疏自编码器学习近似独立的潜在特征；通过雅可比向量积解决潜在特征上影响力的不可分解问题；利用Token激活模式将潜在属性传播回输入空间；采用高效逆Hessian近似实现可扩展性。

Result: 在医疗基准测试上，该方法能够识别出稀疏、可解释的Token集合，这些Token共同影响模型预测结果。

Conclusion: 该框架提升了模型在医疗等高风险领域的信任度和可审计性，为需要透明和可问责决策的应用提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: A critical step for reliable large language models (LLMs) use in healthcare is to attribute predictions to their training data, akin to a medical case study. This requires token-level precision: pinpointing not just which training examples influence a decision, but which tokens within them are responsible. While influence functions offer a principled framework for this, prior work is restricted to autoregressive settings and relies on an implicit assumption of token independence, rendering their identified influences unreliable. We introduce a flexible framework that infers token-level influence through a latent mediation approach for general prediction tasks. Our method attaches sparse autoencoders to any layer of a pretrained LLM to learn a basis of approximately independent latent features. Unlike prior methods where influence decomposes additively across tokens, influence computed over latent features is inherently non-decomposable. To address this, we introduce a novel method using Jacobian-vector products. Token-level influence is obtained by propagating latent attributions back to the input space via token activation patterns. We scale our approach using efficient inverse-Hessian approximations. Experiments on medical benchmarks show our approach identifies sparse, interpretable sets of tokens that jointly influence predictions. Our framework enhances trust and enables model auditing, generalizing to high-stakes domain requiring transparent and accountable decisions.

</details>


### [180] [Training Large Language Models to Predict Clinical Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12817)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Foresight Learning扩展到临床预测领域，通过从MIMIC-III时间序列病历中自动构建包含患者历史、未来事件问题和标注标签的预测样本，使用LoRA微调模型，在多种临床任务上显著优于基础提示模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床笔记包含患者随时间演变的丰富信息，但如何将这些信号转化为可用于训练临床预测模型的监督信号具有挑战性，现有方法依赖手工特征或特定终点的分类器。

Method: 将Foresight Learning应用于临床：从MIMIC-III时间序病历中，将患者过去上下文、关于未来可能事件的自然语言问题以及从后续文档解析的标签组合成预测样本，生成6,900个样本覆盖药物、手术、器官支持、微生物学和死亡率等任务，使用LoRA适配器进行微调。

Result: LoRA微调模型将期望校准误差从0.1269降至0.0398，Brier分数从0.199降至0.145，在保留问题上略优于GPT-5点估计。

Conclusion: 该方法能够从纵向病历中生成可复用的临床预测监督信号，无需手工特征或特定终点的分类器，实现有效的临床预测。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical notes contain rich evidence of how patients evolve over time, but converting this signal into training supervision for clinical prediction remains challenging. We extend Foresight Learning to clinical prediction by converting time-ordered MIMIC-III notes into examples consisting of past patient context, a natural-language question about a possible future event, and a label resolved from later documentation. This process yields 6,900 prediction examples from 702 admissions across medications, procedures, organ support, microbiology, and mortality. A small LoRA adapter trained on these examples improves over the prompted base model, reducing expected calibration error from 0.1269 to 0.0398 and Brier score from 0.199 to 0.145, while slightly outperforming GPT-5 point estimates on held-out questions. The approach enables reusable clinical prediction supervision from longitudinal notes without hand-engineered structured features or endpoint-specific classifiers.

</details>


### [181] [Hessian Matching for Machine-Learned Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12823)
*Sanya Murdeshwar,Sanjit Shashi,Kevin Bachelor,William Noid,Ashwin Lokapally,Razvan Marinescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种随机Hessian-向量积匹配框架，在粗粒度分子动力学势能训练中引入二阶曲率信息，在不构建完整Hessian矩阵的情况下，通过预计算投影AA Hessian和在线计算协方差修正项实现高效训练，在9个快速折叠蛋白基准测试中，8个蛋白的慢模式指标优于传统力匹配方法，最大蛋白的KL散度降低达85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的粗粒度神经势能通过力匹配训练只能捕获自由能曲面的梯度信息，而无法约束其曲率（二阶信息），导致模拟精度受限。作者希望引入二阶曲率信息来提高粗粒度势能的准确性和可迁移性。

Method: 1) 在力匹配基础上增加随机Hessian-向量积匹配项；2) 将目标CG Hessian分解为模型无关的投影AA Hessian（训练前单次预计算）和模型相关的协方差修正项（在线计算，额外开销可忽略）；3) 使用随机探测向量构建Hessian匹配目标的无偏随机估计量。

Result: 在9个未见过的快速折叠蛋白基准上，HVP匹配方法在8个蛋白的慢模式指标上优于纯力匹配，最大蛋白的最慢集体模式上KL散度降低高达85%。

Conclusion: 高阶物理监督是构建更准确、更具可迁移性的粗粒度生物分子模拟势能的实用路径。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics enables simulations of atomic systems such as biomolecules at timescales inaccessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but existing CG neural potentials trained via force matching capture only the gradient of the free-energy surface, leaving its curvature unconstrained. We introduce a framework that augments force matching with stochastic Hessian-vector product (HVP) matching, instilling second-order curvature information into CG potentials without constructing the full Hessian. We derive a decomposition of the target CG Hessian into a model-independent projected AA Hessian, precomputed once before training, and a model-dependent covariance correction computed online at negligible cost. We construct an unbiased stochastic estimator of the Hessian-matching objective by using random probe vectors. We evaluate our method by comparing against force matching on a benchmark of nine fast-folding proteins unseen during training. HVP matching outperforms plain force matching on 8 of 9 proteins on slow-mode metrics, with reductions of up to 85% in the Kullback--Leibler divergence between the CG and reference distributions along the slowest collective mode of the largest protein.
