Full-rank training settles into a sharper basin than low-rank methods along random directions, while the reverse holds for the top-1 PCA direction. Each method converges to a geometrically distinct basin. Low-rank activations diverge from full-rank in later layers as training progresses, with GaLore tracking full-rank most closely. Further, validation perplexity does not translate to downstream performance at every scale. Adding geometric and spectral metrics improves the prediction.

</details>


### [259] [Effective Context in Transformers: An Analysis of Fragmentation and Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13485)
*Amirmehdi Jafari Fesharaki,Mohammadamin Rami,Aslan Tchamkerten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限上下文Transformer中，序列表示方式（字节、字符、子词）如何影响预测性能。作者发现：较小的表示单元（如字节/字符）即使扩大上下文窗口也会因"碎片化"导致性能下降；而贪心分词（如BPE）可使较短token窗口等效于较长源上下文窗口。论文建立了有限上下文信息论框架来解释这些现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在固定上下文窗口下，相同数据可以用不同表示方式（字节、字符、子词token）编码。虽然这些表示在信息上是无损的，但它们暴露给模型的信息量不同。核心问题是：表示方式的选择如何影响有限上下文预测器能达到的性能？

Method: 在马尔可夫源上从两个方向研究：(1) 定义"碎片化"（fragmentation）——将每个源符号替换为多个更小单元的无损重编码，证明碎片化会严格增加最优有限上下文对数损失；(2) 研究贪心分词（BPE、WordPiece等），给出token窗口保持源上下文长度的理论保证，该保证取决于token窗口可靠覆盖源历史的能力和分词器的压缩率。

Result: 证明碎片化会严格增加最优有限上下文对数损失，解释了ByT5、CANINE等字节/字符级模型与子词分词模型之间的性能差距。同时证明贪心分词能使短token窗口行为类似于长源上下文窗口，并提供了可实现的损失保证。

Conclusion: 建立了有限上下文信息论框架，用于推理Transformer中表示选择问题，并为实际分词器提供简单诊断方法：测量固定token窗口可靠包含的源上下文量。

Abstract: Transformers predict over a representation of a sequence. The same data can be written as bytes, characters, or subword tokens, and these representations may be lossless. Yet, under a fixed context window, they need not expose the same information to the model. This raises a basic question: how does the choice of representation change what a finite-context predictor can achieve?
  We study this question on Markov sources and uncover two complementary phenomena. First, we observe that moving to smaller representation units can hurt prediction even when the context window is enlarged to cover the relevant source history. To explain this, we introduce fragmentation: a lossless recoding that replaces each source symbol by several smaller units. We prove that fragmentation can strictly increase the optimal finite-context log-loss, showing that the gap is not merely an optimization or capacity issue, but can be intrinsic to the representation. This gives a theoretical account of the finite-context gap observed in byte- and character-level models such as ByT5 and CANINE relative to subword-tokenized models. Second, we study the opposite direction: greedy tokenization -- BPE, WordPiece, and related methods -- which groups source symbols into larger units. We show that tokenization can make a short token window behave like a longer source-context window, and we give a loss guarantee describing when this is achievable. The guarantee depends on how reliably token windows span the needed source history, together with the compression rate of the tokenizer. This also yields a simple diagnostic for real tokenizers: measuring how much source context a fixed token window reliably contains. Together, the two directions establish a finite-context information-theoretic framework for reasoning about representation choices in Transformers.

</details>


### [260] [Path-independent Flow Matching for Multi-parameter Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13487)
*Francisco Téllez,AmirHossein Zamani,Philippe Martin,Shuang Ni,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Sina Sanjari,Yanlei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出路径无关流匹配（PiFM）方法，通过将标准流匹配推广到多参数高维领域，实现分布间路径无关的传输，并在理论上逼近Wasserstein重心，实验表明其在路径无关轨迹插值和分布外样本生成任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配仅支持单参数路径依赖的传输，无法处理多参数变化场景下需要的路径无关性（即变换结果仅取决于初始和目标分布，而非具体路径），这限制了其在需要组合变换一致性和分布插值等任务中的应用。

Method: PiFM通过学习满足结构条件的向量场，将流匹配推广到更高维的参数域；提出易于训练的、免模拟的目标函数，对多参数条件概率路径进行回归。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，PiFM在路径无关轨迹插值和分布外样本生成任务中优于其他方法。

Conclusion: PiFM有效实现了分布间的路径无关传输，并能逼近Wasserstein重心，为分布插值提供了新的理论联系和实用工具。

Abstract: Flow Matching is a powerful framework for learning transport maps between probability distributions. Yet its standard single-parameter formulation is not designed to capture multi-parameter variations where the resulting transport should be path-independent. Path independence is crucial because it ensures that transformations depend only on the initial and target distributions, not on the specific path. In this work, we introduce Path-independent Flow Matching (PiFM), a method for learning vector fields whose induced flows yield path-independent transport between distributions. We show that PiFM generalizes Flow Matching to higher-dimensional parameter domains while enforcing structural conditions that ensure consistency of composed transformations. In addition, we show that, under suitable assumptions, PiFM approximates the Wasserstein barycenter, linking the framework to a notion of distributional interpolation. To enable practical training, we propose a tractable, simulation-free objective that regresses onto multi-parameter conditional probability paths. We showcase empirically that PiFM outperforms other approaches on both synthetic and real world data in interpolating path-independent trajectories and generating desired out of distribution samples.

</details>


### [261] [A Hierarchical Language Model with Predictable Scaling Laws and Provable Benefits of Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687)
*Jason Gaitonde,Frederic Koehler,Elchanan Mossel,Joonhyung Shin,Allan Sly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有层次结构的合成语言（树上的广播过程），通过精确k-gram假设替代上下文长度为k的Transformer，理论证明：对于任何亚线性上下文，自回归生成会产生统计偏差（Ising过程）或生成无效序列（coloring过程），因此需要Ω(n)的上下文长度才能忠实采样长度为n的序列；而仅需Θ(log n)工作记忆的自回归推理模型就能精确采样——呈指数级改进。实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了精确分析自回归生成中上下文长度和推理的作用，作者引入了一类具有层次结构的合成语言，使得可以在理论上严格刻画上下文长度对生成质量的影响。

Method: 提出"精确k-gram假设"替代Transformer；基于此导出渐进预测；在Ising广播过程（软约束）和coloring广播过程（硬约束）两种设定下进行理论分析；并用Transformer实验验证。

Result: (1) Ising过程：生成序列的方差与上下文深度呈对数线性关系，峰度收敛到高斯分布，均偏离真实分布。(2) Coloring过程（冻结区）：有界上下文自回归生成的序列高概率与任何有效着色不一致。(3) 需要Ω(n)上下文长度才能忠实采样。(4) 仅需Θ(log n)工作记忆的推理模型可精确采样。

Conclusion: 上下文长度与推理能力之间存在根本性权衡：缺乏足够上下文的纯自回归生成无法忠实地从层次结构语言中采样，而引入推理（工作记忆）可以指数级降低所需上下文长度。

Abstract: We introduce a family of synthetic languages with hierarchical structure -- generated by a broadcast process on trees -- for which the role of context length and reasoning in autoregressive generation can be analyzed precisely. At the heart of our analytic approach is an \emph{exact $k$-gram ansatz} in place of transformers with context length $k$, a substitution we then validate empirically. Using this ansatz we derive explicit asymptotic predictions for distributional statistics of the sequences produced by a trained model, instantiated in two settings. For the \emph{Ising broadcast process} (a soft-constrained language), we prove that the variance of the generated sum scales log-linearly in the context depth and its kurtosis converges to that of a Gaussian -- both deviating from the true language for any sublinear context. For the \emph{coloring broadcast process} (a hard-constrained language) in the freezing regime, bounded-context autoregression produces sequences that, with high probability, are inconsistent with \emph{any} valid coloring of the underlying tree. Together these results imply an $Ω(n)$ lower bound on the context length required to faithfully sample length-$n$ sequences. In contrast, we prove that an autoregressive \emph{reasoning} model with only $Θ(\log n)$ working memory can sample exactly from the true language -- an exponential improvement. We confirm both the lower-bound predictions and the reasoning-based upper bound empirically with transformers trained on the synthetic language; the trained models track our asymptotic predictions quantitatively across a wide range of context sizes.

</details>


### [262] [Spatiotemporal downscaling and nowcasting of urban land surface temperatures with deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13566)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合静止轨道和极轨道卫星数据，利用U-Net模型将SEVIRI/MSG的3公里分辨率LST降尺度至1公里，再通过ConvLSTM实现15-75分钟的LST预测，在1公里/15分钟分辨率下达到RMSE 1.92°C（降尺度）和0.57-1.15°C（预测）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星LST产品存在空间分辨率与时间分辨率不可兼得的根本性权衡，亟需一种能同时提供高时空分辨率LST数据的方法。

Method: 1) 使用U-Net模型将SEVIRI/MSG（3公里，15分钟）LST映射到MODIS（1公里，每日4次）LST进行空间降尺度；2) 基于ConvLSTM架构对降尺度后的LST场进行15-75分钟时效的临近预报。

Result: 降尺度模型在测试集上RMSE=1.92°C，MBE=0.01°C；预报模型在15-75分钟时效内RMSE为0.57-1.15°C，偏差-0.1至0.14°C，优于持久性和气候滚动中位数基准。

Conclusion: 该高时空分辨率LST预报模型可直接应用于业务化卫星LST监测，有效解决了时空分辨率权衡问题。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a key variable for various applications, such as urban climate and ecology studies. Yet, existing satellite-derived LST products provide either high spatial or high temporal resolution, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between the two. To address this trade-off, we combine observations from a geostationary and a polar orbiting satellite and provide LST fields at high spatial and high temporal resolution (1 km at 15-min intervals). We demonstrate their application for intraday forecasting of LSTs. To estimate LST fields at high spatiotemporal resolution, a U-Net model is trained to map LST fields from SEVIRI/MSG (3 km and 15 min resolution) to LST fields from Terra/Aqua MODIS (1 km, 4 overpasses per day) that are collocated in space and time. The presented model has been trained on LSTs across large European cities with a population exceeding 1 million inhabitants, and achieves an RMSE = $1.92$°C and near-zero bias MBE = $0.01$°C on the hold-out test set. As a second step, we present an LST nowcasting model based on ConvLSTM architecture, trained across downscaled LST fields with forecast lead times of 15 to 75 minutes. The nowcasting model outperforms a persistence and a Climatological Rolling Median benchmarks, with RMSEs of $0.57$ to $1.15$°C for the considered lead times and biases ranging from $-0.1$ to $0.14$°C. An additional validation conducted against independent MODIS overpasses confirms robust performance. Our LST forecast model at high spatiotemporal resolution is directly applicable to operational satellite-based LST monitoring.

</details>


### [263] [High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768)
*Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于协方差矩阵的量化矩阵乘法，并将其应用于大语言模型的权重量化。通过水填充（waterfilling）方法改进GPTQ算法，分析了WaterSIC方案在高速率下的性能，发现其与信息论极限的差距很小，且GPTQ在随机旋转下也接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM量化算法（如GPTQ）在分配量化比特率时采用均匀分配方式，未考虑各坐标的重要性差异。本文旨在利用加权均方误差源编码中的水填充方法，优化比特率分配，提升量化性能。

Method: 利用协方差矩阵Σ_X的信息，将权重量化问题建模为加权均方误差源编码问题，采用经典的水填充（waterfilling）方法分配比特率；分析WaterSIC方案（仅使用标量INT量化器）的高速率性能，并与GPTQ进行对比。

Result: WaterSIC方案的高速率性能与基无关（仅由Σ_X的行列式决定），对随机旋转具有免疫性；其性能与信息论极限的差距在0.25 bit/entry以内。GPTQ在随机旋转下，性能与WaterSIC差距在0.1 bit以内，表明其在高速率下也接近最优。

Conclusion: 水填充方法可以改进实际LLM量化算法；WaterSIC方案具有基无关性和接近最优的性能；GPTQ配合随机旋转也能达到近最优的量化效果。

Abstract: This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs.
  Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.

</details>


### [264] [MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779)
*Mind Lab,:,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Hongquan Gu,Aaron Guan,Nolan Ho,Mutian Hong,Hailee Hou,Peixuan Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Andrew Lei,Kyrie Lei,Alexy Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Theo Li,Zhihui Li,Jiayi Lin,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Xiang Liu,Irvine Lu,Maeve Luo,Runze Lv,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Anson Qiu,Vincent Wang,Rio Yang,Maxwell Yao,Carrie Ye,Regis Ye,Wenlin Ye,Josh Ying,Danney Zeng,Yuhan Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Ruijia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Ya Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Xinyue Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MinT是一个用于LoRA后训练和在线服务的托管基础设施系统，能在共享的基础模型上管理百万级LoRA策略目录，支持从训练到服务的全流程自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多个LoRA策略场景下需要分别为每个策略物化完整的合并检查点，成本高昂且效率低下。MinT旨在解决多策略共享基础模型时的资源浪费和编排复杂度问题。

Method: MinT采用三轴扩展方法：Scale Up支持前沿规模稠密和MoE架构的LoRA强化学习；Scale Down通过仅传输适配器实现高效切换（适配器大小不足基础模型的1%）；Scale Out分离策略寻址与计算工作集，支持百万级可寻址策略目录和千级活跃适配器。

Result: 适配器仅传输在4B稠密模型上提速18.3倍，30B MoE模型上提速2.85倍；并发多策略GRPO缩短墙钟时间1.77倍和1.45倍；打包的MoE LoRA张量使引擎加载速度提升8.5-8.7倍；单引擎可扫描100K策略。

Conclusion: MinT成功实现了在共享的万亿参数级基础模型上训练和服务百万规模LoRA策略目录的管理能力。

Abstract: We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.

</details>


### [265] [ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789)
*Kaiwen Shi,Carlos Oliver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ensembits，首个能够对蛋白质构象系综进行token化的方法，通过残差VQ-VAE和帧蒸馏目标在分子动力学语料上训练，在动态预测、功能预测和零样本突变效应预测等任务上优于现有静态tokenizer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质结构tokenizer仅能捕捉静态结构的局部几何信息，忽略了蛋白质构象系综所揭示的相关运动与替代构象状态。为了将动力学信息引入蛋白质语言模型和设计，需要一种能够tokenize蛋白质构象系综的方法。

Method: 采用残差VQ-VAE架构，结合帧蒸馏目标（frame distillation objective）在大型分子动力学语料上训练。设计上解决了三个核心挑战：跨构象的信息几何描述子推导、变尺寸系综的置换不变编码、以及动力学数据的稀疏性问题。

Result: 在RMSF预测上优于所有相关方法；在基于token的ANOVA检验中，是运动幅度预测最强的独立结构tokenizer；在EC、GO、结合位点/亲和力预测以及零样本突变效应预测上，匹配或超过静态tokenizer，且使用的预训练数据远少于后者。

Conclusion: Ensembits通过蒸馏目标实现了从单张预测结构预测动力学token的能力，缓解了动力学数据稀疏问题。为蛋白质语言建模和设计提供了将动力学纳入考虑的离散词汇表，推动了从静态结构预测向系综生成的发展。

Abstract: Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude. Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.

</details>


### [266] [Di-BiLPS: Denoising induced Bidirectional Latent-PDE-Solver under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13790)
*Zhonghao Li,Chaoyu Liu,Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Di-BiLPS框架，结合VAE、潜空间扩散模型和对比学习，在极稀疏观测数据（低至3%）下高效求解PDE正问题和逆问题，并实现零样本超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中观测数据极度稀疏，限制了传统数值求解器和现有神经方法在PDE建模中的适用性，现有方法在极稀疏场景下精度严重下降且高分辨率推理效率有限。

Method: 提出Di-BiLPS框架：1) VAE压缩高维输入到紧凑潜空间；2) 潜扩散模块建模不确定性；3) 对比学习对齐表示；4) 基于方差保持扩散过程的PDE-informed去噪算法提升推理效率。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，极稀疏输入（低至3%）下达到SOTA性能，同时显著降低计算成本，并实现零样本超分辨率。

Conclusion: Di-BiLPS是一个统一的神经框架，能有效处理极稀疏观测下的PDE正问题和逆问题，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) are fundamental for modeling complex natural and physical phenomena. In many real-world applications, however, observational data are extremely sparse, which severely limits the applicability of both classical numerical solvers and existing neural approaches. While neural methods have shown promising results under moderately sparse observations, their inference efficiency at high resolutions is limited, and their accuracy degrades substantially in the extremely sparse regime. In this work, we propose the Di-BiLPS, a unified neural framework that effectively handle both forward and inverse PDE problems under extremely sparse observations. Di-BiLPS combines a variational autoencoder to compress high-dimensional inputs into a compact latent space, a latent diffusion module to model uncertainty, and contrastive learning to align representations. Operating entirely in this latent space, the framework achieves efficient inference while retaining flexible input-output mapping. In addition, we introduce a PDE-informed denoising algorithm based on a variance-preserving diffusion process, which further improves inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on multiple PDE benchmarks demonstrate that Di-BiLPS consistently achieves SOTA performance under extremely sparse inputs (as low as 3%), while substantially reducing computational cost. Moreover, Di-BiLPS enables zero-shot super-resolution, as it allows predictions over continuous spatial-temporal domains.

</details>


### [267] [Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13801)
*Deepak Pandita,Flip Korn,Chris Welty,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI模型评估中的可重复性危机，提出了一种多层次自助法（multi-level bootstrapping）来建模标注者行为，并分析了在达到统计显著性时，标注项数量（N）与每项标注次数（K）之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的评估面临可重复性危机，人工标注者引入的偏差和主观意见导致实验结果难以复现，而目前缺乏研究标注者数量如何影响实验可重复性的数据。

Method: 提出多级自助法（multi-level bootstrapping）建模标注者行为，利用包含大量评分和持久标注者标识的数据集，分析N（项目数量）与K（每项响应数量）的权衡。

Result: 通过所提方法分析了达到统计显著性时，项目数量与每项标注数量的最优权衡关系。

Conclusion: 标准化评估实践中常用的少量标注（3-5个）可能不足以实现可靠的可重复性，需更合理地配置N和K以达到统计显著性。

Abstract: As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.

</details>


### [268] [Achieving $ε^{-2}$ Sample Complexity for Single-Loop Actor-Critic under Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13639)
*Ishaq Hamza,Zaiwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了离策略演员-评论家方法在强化学习中的最后迭代收敛速率，在单循环、单时间尺度实现下，首次证明了在最小假设（存在诱导不可约马尔可夫链的策略）下达到ε-最优策略的Õ(ε⁻²)样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中Õ(ε⁻²)样本复杂度仅能通过嵌套循环更新或强算法依赖假设（如均匀混合、均匀探索）实现，本文希望在更弱假设下实现相同复杂度。

Method: 采用耦合Lyapunov漂移框架，分别建立演员的几何收敛率和评论家的Õ(1/T)收敛率，通过交叉支配性质将两种Lyapunov漂移不等式结合。

Result: 首次在最小假设下证明了单循环、单时间尺度离策略演员-评论家方法的Õ(ε⁻²)样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该耦合Lyapunov漂移分析框架具有独立价值，可能适用于其他具有无界迭代的耦合迭代算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for off-policy actor--critic methods in reinforcement learning. In particular, under a single-loop, single-timescale implementation and a broad class of policy updates, including approximate policy iteration and natural policy gradient methods, we prove the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity guarantee for finding an $ε$-optimal policy under minimal assumptions, namely, the existence of a policy that induces an irreducible Markov chain. This stands in stark contrast to the existing literature, where an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity is achieved only through nested-loop updates and/or under strong, algorithm-dependent assumptions on the policies, such as uniform mixing and uniform exploration. Technically, to address the challenges posed by the coupled update equations arising from the single-loop implementation, as well as the potentially unbounded iterates induced by off-policy learning, our analysis is based on a coupled Lyapunov drift framework. Specifically, we establish a geometric convergence rate for the actor and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$ convergence rate for the critic, and combine the two Lyapunov drift inequalities through a cross-domination property. We believe this analytical framework is of independent interest and may be applicable to other coupled iterative algorithms with unbounded

</details>


### [269] [Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13834)
*Dongzhe Zheng,Tao Zhong,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数空间视角研究几何网格上的物理场方程求解算子，揭示了Hodge正交性如何通过将不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学分离来解决频谱干扰问题，提出了Hodge谱对偶（HSD）架构，在几何图上实现了更优的精度和物理不变性保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理几何网格上的物理场方程时，频谱干扰问题导致拓扑自由度与几何动力学相互纠缠，难以实现高效且保结构的学习。

Method: 基于Hodge理论和算子分裂，推导出算子级分解；利用离散微分形式捕捉拓扑主导成分，用正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学；构建混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构，引入代数层级的归纳偏置——Hodge谱对偶（HSD）。

Result: 在几何图上实现了更高的精度和效率，并显著增强了对物理不变量的保真度。

Conclusion: Hodge正交性能够有效分离拓扑与几何成分，HSD框架为物理场方程求解提供了一种结构保持且高效的学习范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we study solution operators of physical field equations on geometric meshes from a function-space perspective. We reveal that Hodge orthogonality fundamentally resolves spectral interference by isolating unlearnable topological degrees of freedom from learnable geometric dynamics, enabling an additive approximation confined to structure-preserving subspaces. Building on Hodge theory and operator splitting, we derive a principled operator-level decomposition. The result is a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian architecture with an algebraic-level inductive bias we call Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD). In our framework, we use discrete differential forms to capture topology-dominated components and an orthogonal auxiliary ambient space to represent complex local dynamics. Our method achieves superior accuracy and efficiency on geometric graphs with enhanced fidelity to physical invariants. Our code is available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality

</details>


### [270] [Multi-Objective and Mixed-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13641)
*Yang Bai,Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Jiahong Zhou,Rongxiang Weng,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RDPO方法，通过幅度感知分位数归一化和马氏白化处理异构相关奖励，提升多任务强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂强化学习环境中，异构奖励分布和相关奖励维度常导致标量优势函数构建不稳定，需设计针对性奖励处理方法。

Method: 1）幅度感知分位数归一化（稳定跨提示优势分配） 2）马氏白化（消除奖励子空间相关性冗余）

Result: 在LongCat-Flash后训练中，RDPO提升了指令跟随、写作质量和困难提示鲁棒性，推理与编码能力保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RDPO有效解决多任务混合奖励场景下的两大失败模式，验证了奖励去相关处理对策略优化的价值。

Abstract: Complex reinforcement learning environments frequently employ multi-task and mixed-reward formulations. In these settings, heterogeneous reward distributions and correlated reward dimensions often destabilize the construction of scalar advantages. To address these challenges, we propose Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization (RDPO), a reward-processing method designed to explicitly target both failure modes. RDPO first utilizes Magnitude-Aware Quantile normalization to stabilize prompt-level advantage allocation across binary, fractional, and continuous rewards. It then applies Mahalanobis whitening within each active reward subspace to mitigate correlation redundancy prior to aggregation. When applied during the post-training of LongCat-Flash, RDPO enhances instruction following, writing quality, and robustness to hard prompts while remaining broadly competitive on reasoning and coding evaluations.

</details>


### [271] [Three-Stage Learning Unlocks Strong Performance in Simple Models for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13678)
*Zhenan Yu,Guangxin Jiang,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAIR是一种分阶段训练范式，通过共享学习、个体微调和残差学习三个渐进阶段，在保持简单时序映射模型（如浅层MLP）结构的同时，显著提升长时序预测性能，在九个基准上达到或超越现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时序预测方法多依赖频域建模、显式分解、多尺度混合或跨变量交互等复杂架构先验，而忽视了简单时序映射模型应该如何训练和组织的问题。作者希望在不引入复杂模块的前提下，充分释放简单模型的能力。

Method: 提出STAIR三阶段训练范式：(1) 共享时序映射学习变量间的共同时序动态；(2) 通过通道级微调将共享模型自适应到每个变量，捕获个体模式；(3) 通过残差学习补充跨变量信息。同时引入Shared-to-Individual微调和alpha-RevIN机制来缓解严格通道独立性和RevIN归一化先验过强的问题。

Result: 在9个长时序预测基准上，STAIR以浅层MLP为核心时序预测器，匹配或超越了近期强基线方法，验证了简单模型通过合理训练范式也能取得优异性能。

Conclusion: STAIR提供了一个简洁而有效的长时序预测视角：不必依赖复杂架构，通过分阶段渐进式训练即可释放简单模型的能力，为长时序预测研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recent studies on long-term time series forecasting have shown that simple linear models and MLP-based predictors can achieve strong performance without increasingly complex architectures. However, many competitive baselines still rely on structural priors such as frequency-domain modeling, explicit decomposition, multi-scale mixing, or sophisticated cross-variable interaction modules, while paying less attention to how simple temporal mappings should be trained and organized. In this paper, we propose STAIR, short for Stagewise Temporal Adaptation via Individualization and Residual Learning, a training paradigm for long-term time series forecasting that aims to unlock the capacity of simple temporal mapping models without introducing complex architectural modules. STAIR decomposes forecasting ability into three progressive stages: it first learns common temporal dynamics across variables through a shared temporal mapping, then adapts the shared model to each variable via channel-wise fine-tuning to capture variable-specific patterns, and finally complements the backbone with cross-variable information through residual learning. We further introduce Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning and alpha-RevIN to mitigate the limitations of strict channel independence and the overly strong normalization prior induced by standard RevIN. This design gradually increases modeling flexibility while keeping the core temporal predictor as a shallow MLP in the main experiments, with linear variants analyzed separately. Experiments on nine long-term forecasting benchmarks show that STAIR matches or outperforms recent strong baselines while preserving a simple temporal backbone, providing a concise and effective modeling perspective for long-term time series forecasting.

</details>


### [272] [Sampling from Flow Language Models via Marginal-Conditioned Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13681)
*Iskander Azangulov,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Flow Language Models (FLMs)的后验预测采样方法，相比标准DDPM采样器，该方法通过从因子化后验分布中采样干净端点并利用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck桥条件化，实现了无需额外训练的质量-多样性权衡改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDPM式采样器会将FLM的边际分布坍缩为单一点（条件均值），但该点通常不是有效的one-hot序列，导致采样质量受限。因此需要一种更自然的采样方法。

Method: 提出后验预测采样器：每个反向步骤中，先从FLM token边际定义的因子化后验中采样干净one-hot端点，再基于该端点从解析的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck桥中采样下一个连续状态。该方法无需训练，与标准采样使用相同模型评估次数，并支持温度缩放和nucleus截断等解码控制。

Result: 证明了在精确后验边际下，端点近似误差等于token位置间的条件多信息；单步桥核保留所有token-wise后验预测边际；通过Girsanov路径空间比较证明边际条件桥的降噪误差项不大于冻结条件均值桥。实验表明该方法改善了质量-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 后验预测采样是FLMs更自然的采样方法，在理论上更优，实践中提升了生成质量与多样性的平衡。

Abstract: Flow Language Models (FLMs) are a recently introduced class of language models which adapt continuous flow matching for one-hot encoded token sequences. Their denoisers have a special structure absent from generic continuous diffusion models: each block of the denoising mean is a posterior marginal distribution over the clean token at that position. Standard DDPM-style samplers collapse these marginals to a single conditional-mean endpoint and bridge toward this simplex-valued point, which is generally not a valid one-hot sequence. We argue that the natural sampler for an FLM is instead posterior-predictive. At each reverse step, we sample a clean one-hot endpoint from the factorized posterior defined by the FLM token marginals, and then sample the next continuous state from the analytic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck bridge conditioned on that endpoint. The method is training-free, uses the same model evaluations as standard sampling, and gives a principled interface for token-level decoding controls such as temperature scaling and nucleus truncation. We show that, under exact posterior marginals, the endpoint approximation error is exactly the conditional multi-information among token positions. The induced one-step bridge kernel preserves all token-wise posterior-predictive marginals and loses only the residual cross-position dependence. Finally, we prove a Girsanov path-space comparison showing that the marginal-conditioned bridge has a no-larger denoising-error term than the frozen conditional-mean bridge, with strict improvement whenever intermediate coordinate-wise bridge observations reveal additional information about the clean token. Experiments with FLMs show that the sampler improves the quality--diversity tradeoff. Code is available at: github.com/imbirik/mcb.

</details>


### [273] [Scale-Sensitive Shattering: Learnability and Evaluability at Optimal Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13684)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Han Shao,Tom Waknine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了实值函数类在尺度γ下的均匀收敛、γ/2下的不可知学习性与各尺度γ'>γ的fat-shattering维数有限性三者间的等价性，解决了Anthony和Bartlett（1999）的猜想，给出了紧的渐近度量熵界，并应用于有界积分概率度量的可评估性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Anthony和Bartlett（1999）关于学习性精确尺度的开放问题，反驳Phil Long关于乘法2-因子差距不可避免的猜想，改进Bartlett和Long（1998）的上界，并回答Alon等人（1997）及Rudelson和Vershynin（2006）的开放问题。

Method: 关键技术是直接对经验ℓ∞覆盖数进行界估计，绕过了标准打包数迂回方法。基于此推导出关于fat-shattering尺度γ的紧渐近度量熵界：在γ/2尺度下得到O(log² n)界，在2γ尺度下得到O(log n)界。

Result: 建立了尺度敏感的PAC学习基本定理推广；获得了紧的渐近度量熵界，且O(log² n)界有时是紧的；对有界积分概率度量建立了精确三分法：每个IPM要么可估计，要么不能在c<3的乘法因子下弱评估，而3-弱评估总是成立。

Conclusion: 解决了多个开放问题，改进了已有上界，建立了最优的定量关系，并给出了在积分概率度量评估中的重要应用。同时提出了样本复杂度和可评估性方面的若干开放问题。

Abstract: We study the optimal scale at which real-valued function classes exhibit uniform convergence and learnability. Our main result establishes a scale-sensitive generalization of the fundamental theorem of PAC learning: for every bounded real-valued class and every $γ>0$, uniform convergence at scale $γ$, agnostic learnability at scale $γ/2$, and finiteness of the fat-shattering dimension at every scale $γ'>γ$ are equivalent. This resolves a question by Anthony and Bartlett (Cambridge Univ. Press 1999) on the precise scales governing learnability, refuting a conjecture attributed there to Phil Long that a multiplicative 2-factor gap is unavoidable, and improves the upper bounds of Bartlett and Long (JCSS 1998), which incur such a loss.
  The key technical ingredient is a direct bound on empirical $\ell_\infty$ covering numbers, avoiding the standard detour through packing numbers. As a consequence, we obtain sharp asymptotic metric-entropy bounds in terms of the fat-shattering scale $γ$: an $O(\log^2 n)$ bound holds already at scale $γ/2$, while an $O(\log n)$ bound holds at scale $2γ$. We further show that the $O(\log^2 n)$ bound is sometimes tight. These results resolve open questions by Alon et al. (JACM 1997) and Rudelson and Vershynin (Ann. of Math. 2006).
  As an application, we establish a sharp dichotomy for bounded integral probability metrics: every such IPM is either estimable or cannot be weakly evaluated within any multiplicative factor $c<3$, while $3$-weak evaluability always holds, resolving an open question from Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026). We also highlight several open questions on quantitative sample complexity and evaluability.

</details>


### [274] [Polyhedral Instability Governs Regret in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13692)
*Yuetai Li,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合动作在线决策问题中遗憾（regret）与多面体不稳定性（区域切换次数）之间的关系，提出了一个统一的理论框架，并证明了遗憾的上界和下界均为 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))，在子模-凹博弈中特化为 Θ(√((1+SC_T) T log n))，实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线组合决策问题通常通过凸松弛转化为在线凸优化，但遗憾界往往依赖于动作空间的维度或显式枚举，忽略了问题内在的几何结构。本文旨在揭示遗憾与多面体区域切换次数（即“多面体不稳定性”）之间的本质关系，从而在低不稳定性的实际场景中提供更紧的界。

Method: 作者定义了多面体不稳定性指标 RS_T（区域切换次数）和 V_max（每个区域的最大顶点数），在完全信息反馈和固定划分假设下，推导了遗憾的上下界。对于在线子模-凹博弈，利用Lovász凸化将问题转化为置换切换次数 SC_T，并给出了匹配的遗憾界。实验在最短路径和影响力最大化问题上验证了理论预测。

Result: 理论结果表明，遗憾界为 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))，在子模-凹博弈中为 Θ(√((1+SC_T) T log n))，实现了从专家学习（低切换）到维度依赖（高切换）的平滑插值。实验显示，实际中低不稳定性的确存在，且遗憾随切换次数线性增长。

Conclusion: 多面体不稳定性是组合在线决策问题中遗憾的核心决定因素，通过区域切换次数可以统一刻画不同算法的表现。这为设计低切换成本的实际算法提供了理论依据，并指出了未来研究高维动作空间下不稳定性度量的方向。

Abstract: Many online decision problems over combinatorial actions are addressed via convex relaxations, leading to online convex optimization with piecewise linear objectives and induced polyhedral structure. We show that regret in such problems is governed by \emph{polyhedral instability}: the number of changes of the active region. Under full information feedback and fixed partition assumptions, if $\mathrm{RS}_T$ denotes the number of region switches and $V_{\max}$ the maximum number of vertices per region, we prove $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{RS}_T)\,T\,\log V_{\max}})$ interpolating between experts-like and dimension-dependent OCO rates. For online submodular--concave games under Lovász convexification, this reduces to the permutation-switch count $\mathrm{SC}_T$, yielding the matching rate $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{SC}_T)\,T\,\log n})$. Experiments on synthetic and real combinatorial problems (shortest path, influence maximization) validate the predicted scaling and indicate that low-instability regimes can arise in practice without explicit enumeration of actions.

</details>


### [275] [MILM: Large Language Models for Multimodal Irregular Time Series with Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13711)
*Hsing-Huan Chung,Shijun Li,Yoav Wald,Xing Han,Suchi Saria,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MILM模型，通过将多模态不规则时间序列转换为XML格式，并采用两阶段微调策略，使LLM能够同时利用采样模式和观测值进行预测，在多个EHR数据集上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态不规则时间序列（MITS）中，观测的不规则时序和通道模式本身携带预测信号，但现有方法未能充分利用这些信息，而LLM具备处理异构数据的潜力。

Method: 将MITS表示为XML格式的时间有序三元组，采用两阶段微调：第一阶段仅在值被遮蔽的MITS上训练以学习采样模式，第二阶段在全量MITS上联合建模采样模式和观测值。

Result: MILM-2S（两阶段）和MILM-Direct（单阶段）在多个EHR数据集上分别取得最佳和第二佳平均性能；在值缺失评估中MILM-2S优势更明显。

Conclusion: 采样模式本身携带预测信号，两阶段训练策略能有效捕捉这些信号，且在值缺失场景下保留时间和通道信息可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Multimodal irregular time series (MITS) consist of asynchronous and irregularly sampled observations from heterogeneous numerical and textual channels. In healthcare, for example, patients' electronic health records (EHR) include irregular lab measurements and clinical notes. The irregular timing and channel patterns of observations carry predictive signal alongside the numerical values and textual content. LLMs are natural candidates for processing such heterogeneous data, given their extensive pretrained knowledge spanning textual and numerical domains. We introduce MILM (Multimodal Irregular time series Language Model), which represents MITS as time-ordered triplets in Extensible Markup Language (XML) format and fine-tunes an LLM through a two-stage strategy for MITS classification. The first stage trains on value-redacted MITS to predict from sampling patterns alone, and the second stage trains on full MITS to jointly model sampling patterns and observed values. Our two-stage model (MILM-2S) and its single-stage counterpart (MILM-Direct) achieve the best and second-best average performance on multiple EHR datasets. Further value redaction evaluations confirm that sampling patterns carry predictive signal and that MILM-2S learns to exploit them. In the value pending evaluation we introduce, where some values are unavailable at prediction time, MILM-2S outperforms MILM-Direct by a larger margin compared to standard evaluation. For MILM-2S, preserving the time and channel of value-pending observations as additional sampling information further improves in-hospital mortality prediction.

</details>


### [276] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Entropic Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13717)
*Amer Essakine,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限horizon下基于熵风险度量的风险敏感强化学习的最优策略识别问题，提出了一种新的前向模型算法，通过利用指数效用函数的平滑性导出更紧的浓度界，并设计新的停止规则，从而将样本复杂度从O(e^{2|β|H})降低至与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，弥合了指数依赖差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在风险敏感强化学习的最佳策略识别中，样本复杂度的下界和上界之间存在指数级差距（下界为Ω(e^{|β|H})，上界为O(e^{2|β|H})），这一额外指数因子源于对指数效用函数过于宽松的浓度控制。

Method: 1) 基于前向模型构建算法，采用KL散度形式的探索奖励；2) 利用指数效用函数的平滑性推导更紧的浓度界；3) 提出新的停止规则以充分利用紧浓度界。

Result: 样本复杂度从O(e^{2|β|H})降低至与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，弥合了指数依赖的理论差距。

Conclusion: 本文提出的前向模型算法通过更紧的浓度控制和优化的停止规则，成功解决了风险敏感强化学习中最优策略识别样本复杂度的指数级开放问题。

Abstract: We study best-policy identification for finite-horizon risk-sensitive reinforcement learning under the entropic risk measure. Recent work established a constant gap in the exponential horizon dependence between lower and upper bounds on the number of samples required to identify an approximately optimal policy. Precisely, known lower bounds scale in $Ω(e^{|β| H})$ where $H$ is the horizon of the MDP, while the state-of-the-art upper bound achieves at best $O(e^{2|β| H})$ (arXiv:2506.00286v2) using a generative model. We show that this extra exponential factor can be traced to overly loose concentration control for exponential utilities. To close this open gap, we revisit the analysis of this problem through a forward-model based algorithm building on KL-based exploration bonuses that we adapt to the entropic criterion. The improvement we get is due to two main novel technical innovations. We leverage the smoothness properties of the exponential utility to derive sharper concentration bounds, and we propose a new stopping rule that exploits further this tightness to obtain a sample complexity that matches the lower bound.

</details>


### [277] [Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13731)
*Lixiang Yan,Samuel Greiff,Jason M. Lodge,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI在教育中的应用虽能提升表现，但未能促进深度认知和元认知加工，从而影响高质量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式AI在教育中使用的局限性，特别是其对深度认知和元认知加工的支持不足。

Method: 基于摘要内容，未明确提及具体方法，可能为理论分析或文献综述。

Result: 生成式AI能提升学习表现，但无法促进高质量学习所需的深度认知和元认知加工。

Conclusion: 当前生成式AI的教育应用存在关键缺陷，需改进以支持深层学习过程。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.

</details>


### [278] [Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13740)
*Valentin Six,Frederik Panse,Mathis Fajeau,Lancelot Da Costa,Mridul Sharma,Alfonso Amayuelas,Tim Z. Xiao,David Hyland,Philipp Hennig,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pinductor方法，利用大语言模型的先验知识，从少量观测-动作轨迹中高效学习部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）世界模型，无需访问隐藏状态信息，在样本效率和性能上匹配甚至超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体需要学习内部世界模型才能有效行动，但传统POMDP学习方法需要大量环境交互。作者希望利用语言模型中的先验知识来减少昂贵的交互成本。

Method: 提出Pinductor框架：LLM作为候选POMDP模型生成器，从少量观测-动作轨迹中提出候选模型，并基于信念似然分数迭代优化模型。

Result: Pinductor在性能和样本效率上匹配甚至超越需要访问隐藏状态的LLM-based POMDP方法，并显著优于表格型POMDP基线。性能随LLM能力提升而提升，并在环境语义信息被隐藏时仍能优雅退化。

Conclusion: 语言模型先验知识是部分可观测环境下样本高效世界模型学习的实用工具，为构建通用智能体迈出了一步。

Abstract: Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.

</details>


### [279] [GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13743)
*Yifan Duan,Siyuan Zheng,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GHGbench是一个面向公司和建筑级别的温室气体排放预测开源数据集与基准，包含32,000+公司年记录和491,591栋建筑年记录，揭示了建筑排放预测更难、分布内外差距远大于模型间差距、多模态遥感嵌入在表格模型泛化失效处起作用等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体级碳排放预测的开放数据集和基准在访问、规模、粒度和评估等方面存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一、全面的评估框架。

Method: 构建了公司追踪（32,000+公司年记录，含Scope 1+2/3披露及金融行业信号）和建筑追踪（491,591建筑年记录，整合13个来源，覆盖26个都市区）两个数据集；定义了分布内、跨区域/城市迁移、时间留出和短期预测等任务；采用梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer、多模态融合等方法，并使用多种子配对自助法进行统计检验。

Result: 三个主要发现：(i) 建筑排放预测比公司排放更难；(ii) 分布内与分布外差距远大于任何模型间差距，表格基础模型首次在多城市建筑排放任务上显著优于调优的树模型；(iii) 多模态遥感嵌入在表格泛化失效时起关键作用。同时发现灾难性的城市迁移和行业因子查找表瓶颈等系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: GHGbench为碳排放预测研究提供了统一的标准数据集和基准，揭示了当前方法的局限性，指明了未来改进方向。

Abstract: Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction. The company track contains 32,000+ company-year records from 12,000+ firms with Scope 1+2 and Scope 3 disclosures and financial/sectoral signals; the building track harmonises 491,591 building-year records from 13 open sources into a single schema across 26 metropolitan areas (10 U.S., 15 Australian, 1 Singaporean), with climate covariates and multimodal remote-sensing embeddings. GHGbench defines canonical splits with in-distribution and cross-region/city transfer as primary tasks and temporal hold-out plus short-horizon forecasting as supplementary appendix evidence; headline baselines span gradient-boosted trees, a tabular foundation model, MLP, FT-Transformer, and multimodal fusion, with an LLM panel as auxiliary, all evaluated under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions; (ii) the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dwarfs any within-model gap across both the company track and the building track, and a tabular foundation model is, to our knowledge, the first baseline to open a paired-bootstrap-significant gap over tuned trees on a multi-city building-emissions task; (iii) multimodal remote-sensing embeddings help precisely where tabular generalisation breaks. GHGbench also exposes catastrophic city transfer and the sector-factor lookup ceiling as systematic failure modes. Code and reconstruction recipes are available at GHGbench.

</details>


### [280] [Fast and effective algorithms for fair clustering at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13759)
*Claudio Mantuano,Manuel Kammermann,Philipp Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向公平聚类的通用框架，通过三种启发式方法精确控制聚类成本与公平性之间的权衡，在保证可扩展性的同时显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平聚类方法对成本-公平性权衡的控制能力有限，且难以兼顾大规模数据的可扩展性与解的质量。

Method: 提出通用框架，并设计了三种启发式方法：第一种侧重解质量和约束灵活性，第二种在保持高质量的同时提升可扩展性，第三种面向最大可扩展性，可在数秒内处理百万级对象。

Result: 在基准数据集上的综合数值实验表明，所提启发式方法在聚类效果和公平性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架和启发式方法能够有效管理公平聚类中的成本-公平性权衡，兼具解质量与可扩展性，代码已公开可复现。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised machine learning task that consists of identifying groups of similar objects. It has numerous applications and is increasingly used in fairness-sensitive domains where objects represent individuals, such as customers, employees, or students. We address a fair clustering problem in which objects belong to protected groups. The problem consists of partitioning the objects into a predefined number of clusters while attaining a user-defined target level of fairness, meaning that each protected group is sufficiently represented in each cluster. The objective is to minimize the clustering cost, defined as the sum of squared Euclidean distances between the objects and the centers of their clusters. Since clustering cost and fairness are generally in conflict, managing the trade-off between them is essential in practical applications. Existing methods provide limited control over this trade-off and either fail to scale to large datasets or, when they scale, produce low-quality solutions. We propose a general framework for fair clustering that provides precise control over the cost-fairness trade-off and introduce three heuristics based on it. The first heuristic focuses on solution quality and the flexibility to incorporate additional constraints, the second improves scalability while retaining high solution quality, and the third is designed for maximum scalability, producing solutions for instances with millions of objects in seconds. The proposed heuristics outperform existing approaches in comprehensive numerical experiments on benchmark datasets. The source code of our heuristics and instructions for reproducing the experiments are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [281] [Attention Once Is All You Need: Efficient Streaming Inference with Stateful Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13784)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数据驱动的流式推理引擎，通过有状态会话和预计算机制，将查询延迟从O(n)降低到O(|q|)，在流式市场数据基准测试中实现最高5.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer推理引擎是请求驱动的，每次查询都需要O(n)的预填充开销。在流式工作负载中，数据持续到达且查询不断增长的上下文，这种开销难以承受。

Method: 提出数据驱动计算模型，核心是有状态会话：持久化KV缓存随新数据增量推进，将预填充移出关键路径；引入Flash Queries机制，利用数据到达间的空闲GPU周期预评估已注册问题并缓存答案；设计多租户连续批处理调度器，包含单元预算准入和前缀感知分组预填充，支持单GPU上数十个有状态会话共存且保持完整二次自注意力。

Result: 在流式市场数据基准测试中，参考实现相比vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、llama.cpp等传统推理引擎实现最高5.9倍加速，且查询延迟随累积上下文增长保持不变。

Conclusion: 数据驱动、有状态的计算模型能从根本上消除流式场景中预填充开销，使查询延迟与上下文大小解耦，并利用空闲周期实现预计算结果，为流式推理提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Conventional transformer inference engines are request-driven, paying an O(n) prefill cost on every query. In streaming workloads, where data arrives continuously and queries probe an ever-growing context, this cost is prohibitive. We introduce a data-driven computational model centred on stateful sessions: a persistent KV cache advanced incrementally as new data arrives, so prefill is moved off the critical path and query latency becomes O(|q|), independent of accumulated context size. Building on this, Flash Queries reclaim idle GPU cycles between data arrivals to pre-evaluate registered questions and return cached answers before the user asks, a pattern that is structurally impossible in stateless engines because they discard intermediate state between requests. A multi-tenant continuous-batching scheduler with cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill lets dozens of stateful sessions coexist on a single GPU while preserving full quadratic self-attention. On streaming market-data benchmarks the reference implementation achieves up to 5.9x speedup over conventional inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp), holding query latency constant as accumulated context grows.

</details>


### [282] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Antepartum Prediction of Pregnancy-Associated Thrombotic Microangiopathy Using Routine Longitudinal Laboratory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13786)
*Chuanchuan Sun,Zhen Yu,Qin Fan,Qingchao Chen,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习模型（梯度提升）分析纵向实验室检测数据，在妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）风险预测中取得较好效果（AUROC 0.872），发现胱抑素C在第6周可作为早期监测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）罕见但致命，早期风险预测困难，因其实验室异常信号微妙、多维且易被妊娠期生理变化掩盖，传统单变量或规则方法难以捕捉复杂风险特征。

Method: 回顾性纳入300例妊娠（142例P-TMA，158例对照），保留146个纵向实验室预测变量。采用分层抽样划分训练集（80%）和测试集（20%），评估5种算法，通过交叉验证AUROC选择梯度提升模型，并在测试集上评估性能。

Result: 梯度提升模型在测试集上AUROC为0.872（95%CI: 0.769-0.952），AUPRC为0.883（95%CI: 0.780-0.959），灵敏度0.750，特异度0.812。

Conclusion: 常规临床纵向实验室检测数据包含可用于P-TMA风险预测的信息性信号，胱抑素C在第6周有望成为早期监测指标。

Abstract: Background: Pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) is rare but life-threatening. Early risk prediction before overt clinical presentation remains challenging, as the associated laboratory abnormalities are subtle, multidimensional, and frequently masked by common physiological changes such as gestational thrombocytopenia and pregnancy-related proteinuria, thus overlapping heavily with benign obstetric and renal conditions. This complexity is poorly captured by univariate or rule-based approaches; however, it is addressable by machine learning, which can extract latent, time-dependent risk signatures from longitudinal clinical tests. Methods: This retrospective study included 300 pregnancies comprising 142 P-TMA cases and 158 controls. After exclusion of identifiers and non-informative variables, 146 longitudinal laboratory predictors were retained. Participants were divided into a training cohort (80%) and a held-out test cohort (20%) using stratified sampling. Five algorithms were evaluated: logistic regression, support vector machine with radial basis function kernel, random forest, extra trees, and gradient boosting. The final model was selected by mean cross-validated AUROC, refitted on the full training cohort, and evaluated once in the held-out test cohort. Interpretability analyses examined global feature importance and distributional patterns of leading predictors. Results: Gradient boosting was prespecified by cross-validation in the training cohort. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.872 (95% CI: 0.769-0.952) and an AUPRC of 0.883 (95% CI: 0.780-0.959) in a held-out test cohort, with sensitivity of 0.750 and specificity of 0.812. Conclusions: Longitudinal clinical laboratory tests obtained during routine care contained informative and clinically plausible signals for P-TMA risk. Notably, cystatin C at week 6 showed promise as an early monitoring indicator.

</details>


### [283] [Force-Aware Neural Tangent Kernels for Scalable and Robust Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13788)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Zachary Weller-Davies,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Shikha Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经正切核（NTK）的主动学习方法，用于机器学习原子间势能（MLIPs），通过分块特征空间后验方差筛选实现线性扩展，并引入力感知NTK，在OC20等数据集上实现了高效、鲁棒的主动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs的主动学习面临三大挑战：候选池规模大导致计算瓶颈、需要同时利用能量和力标签、候选池分布偏移时鲁棒性不足。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 提出分块特征空间后验方差筛选框架（线性扩展，避免核矩阵显式存储），将神经正切核（NTK）扩展为力感知NTK和联合能量-力NTK，用于向量场预测的相似性度量。

Result: 在OC20数据集上，联合能量-力NTK在所有指标和分布划分上取得最低的能量和力MAE/RMSE；在T1x、PMechDB、RGD基准上，力NTK方法媲美基线且比基于委员会的方法更高效；在分布偏移场景下鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 单个预训练MLIP通过NTK方法可实现可扩展、力感知、分布鲁棒的主动学习，为基础模型微调提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Active learning for machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) must address several challenges to be practical: scaling to large candidate pools, leveraging energy-force supervision, and maintaining robustness when candidate pools are biased relative to the target distribution. In this work, we jointly address these challenges. We first introduce a linearly scaling acquisition framework based on chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting. By avoiding materialisation of the candidate and train set kernels, this approach enables screening of ~200k structures within hours and applies broadly to acquisition strategies that score candidates based on molecular similarity metrics. We then extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware setting via mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, yielding a force NTK and a joint energy-force NTK that provide natural similarity metrics for vector-field prediction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the joint energy-force NTK on the OC20 dataset, where force-aware acquisition is crucial: it achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all metrics and distribution splits. Across T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, our force NTK methods remain competitive with established baselines while being significantly more efficient than committee-based approaches. Under a controlled candidate-pool shift case study on T1x, acquisition based on pretrained MLIP embeddings and NTKs remains robust, whereas committee-based methods exhibit higher variance. Overall, these results show that a single pretrained MLIP can enable scalable, force-aware, and distribution-robust active learning for foundation-model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [284] [Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13810)
*Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Dmitry Krachun,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用单次随机Hadamard变换的抖动量化方法，证明了该方法在均方误差界上渐近匹配真实随机旋转矩阵，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集随机旋转虽理论保证好但计算复杂度高（Θ(d²)），随机Hadamard变换（O(d log d)）虽降低计算成本但离散结构导致理论分析困难、压缩保证较弱。本文旨在弥合高效计算与强理论保证之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用单次随机Hadamard变换（HD）对输入向量进行变换，减去随机标量偏移后再进行标量量化（抖动量化），在几乎零额外成本下注入额外随机性。

Result: 证明了该抖动量化方法是无偏的，其均方误差界渐近匹配真实随机旋转矩阵。特别地，抖动版本的TurboQuant在每坐标b比特下达到MSE = (π√3/2 + o(1))·4^{-b}，其中o(1)项对所有单位向量和所有维度一致地随量化级数增长而消失。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙引入抖动机制，使离散结构的Hadamard变换也能获得与完全随机旋转相当的理论保证，在不显著增加计算开销的前提下实现了高效且理论完备的向量量化。

Abstract: Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees.
  In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.

</details>


### [285] [Uncertainty-Driven Anomaly Detection for Psychotic Relapse Using Smartwatches: Forecasting and Multi-Task Learning Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13816)
*Nikolaos Tsalkitzis,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos,Niki Efthymiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于智能手表的日常精神病复发检测框架，并通过后期融合策略将两者结合，在公开数据集上取得了优于竞赛冠军基线8%的改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字表型技术能够连续被动监测行为和生理信号，有望实现精神病复发的早期检测，但现有方法在真实世界可穿戴设备变异性下的鲁棒性不足，且缺乏对心脏、运动和睡眠等多模态信号的系统融合研究。

Method: 方法包括两个框架：一是基于心脏动力学预测的异常检测框架（预测-观测偏差作为异常指标），二是多任务学习框架（融合睡眠、运动和心脏信号，学习时间感知嵌入并预测测量时序）。两者均采用Transformer编码器和集成MLP估计预测不确定性，最终通过后期融合策略将两个框架的异常信号联合为统一决策分数。

Result: 在2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge数据集上，融合模型相对竞赛冠军基线实现了8%的改进，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合心脏、运动和睡眠等多种数字表型对于高保真度地检测真实环境中的精神病复发至关重要。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping enables continuous passive monitoring of behavior and physiology, offering a promising paradigm for early detection of psychotic relapse. In this work, we develop and systematically study two smartwatch-based frameworks for daily relapse detection. The first forecasts cardiac dynamics and flags deviations between predicted and observed features as indicators of abnormality. The second adopts a multi-task formulation that fuses sleep with motion and cardiac-derived signals, learning time-aware embeddings and predicting measurement timing. Both pipelines use Transformer encoders and output a daily anomaly score, derived from predictive uncertainty estimated via an ensemble of multilayer perceptrons to improve robustness to real-world wearable variability. While each framework independently demonstrates strong predictive power, we show that they capture complementary physiological signatures. Consequently, we propose a late-fusion strategy that synergistically combines the anomaly signals from both architectures into a unified decision score. We benchmark our methodology on the 2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge dataset, where our fused model achieves a 8% relative improvement over the competition-winning baseline. Our results, supported by extensive ablation studies, suggest that the integration of diverse digital phenotypes, cardiac, motion, and sleep, is essential for the high-fidelity detection of psychotic relapse in real-world settings.

</details>
